And opened my eyes with torchlight so bright  
And now with ointment and gently you make them all right  
You came into this world, 'though you it can't touch  
For you walk with your lotus feet; may I bathe in their dust?  
You have opened my eyes, my wounds you do heal  
O let me be yours, I must take the cure

O Śrīla Prabhupāda  
from habit I run  
trying to catch māyā, attempt to have fun  
And I would plunge into darkness but for you, Brilliant Sun.

A light of my life, but a candle I bring  
to place at your feet; but let the world ring,  
With your glorious message, turn from wanton ways  
and let all sing and rejoice this most happy day  
To let this be our work, let his be our play,  
may they all sing Hare Kṛṣṇa on your next birthday.

Please engage me  
in your loving service

Your most fallen and insignificant,

## Ahmedabad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Long are we ever seeking the dust of your lotus feet, for your infinite mercy holds no bounds. In your eyes you see only Kṛṣṇa, but in ours we see only lust. By lust and hate we are bound to this world, seeing no light day after day. Then as if the sun had burned away the cloud you freely gave us Kṛṣṇa, that transcendental sound. From that day on our lives have changed, never to be the same. Then you taught us compassion and taught us to spread this holy name.

So now we are in the desert, where the material flame is burning bright. We're surrounded by a desert that is poison to our sight. But deep within this desert lies an oasis that glitters more than gold. Its taste is like that of nectar and its touch is softer than snow. This oasis is your blessing, which forever seems to grow. Please always let us follow you, for you are the only retreat. Sometimes we find it difficult, and it seems like the fire is too hot, but if we can just learn to take shelter of your lotus feet, we know that you will free us from this troubled knot.

"I do not find the strength to carry on alone the *saṅkīrtana* of the holy name of Hari. Please give me your blessing, just one drop of faith with which to obtain the holy name of Kṛṣṇa." Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura sings like this and we are trying to understand his words. Please let us understand his words. Please let us understand this holy name so that the lust will fall away. Please give us your soothing embrace so that we may carry on. This is what we ask for, that will be our faith.

To trouble you with our imperfections in not what we desire, but we are fallen fools and know nothing but your grace. To beg, to borrow, to steal like a madman in the night, seeking after *prema-bhakti,* yet our hands are bound so tight, bound by the darkness of ignorance. O Prabhupāda, teach us to surrender more. We fall down just like a rod trying to catch your lotus feet.

As a lotus flower opens, more of its beauty is revealed. As we are learning to surrender, your purity we can feel. How is it possible for you to do so much? There are no limits to your devotion, there are no boundaries in your way.

Like an iceberg in the ocean our hearts have frozen tight. But your words are like the sunshine bringing warmth and light. How can we understand your will, for it is of the Divine. Your words are ever fresh to us as the sun does ever shine. Taking off our shackles of illusion and carrying us back home, there is no way our hearts won't melt, just as from one small seed come flowers and fruits.

Our words seem incomplete in describing what you hold. We are falling at your lotus feet, desiring more and more to be enthusiastic, to carry out this chore. We are lame and blind and foolish in our ways, bowing down before you, praying for your grace.

*ISKCON Ahmedabad*

## Amsterdam

The spiritual master is as much honored as Kṛṣṇa as His most confidential servant.  
The spiritual master is the ocean of mercy and the representative of the ocean of truth.  
The spiritual master is admired by the greatest demigods and is factually served by them.  
The spiritual master is preaching the everlasting message of Lord Caitanya.  
The spiritual master is the hand of God.  
The spiritual master is blessing the sincere disciple with genuine spiritual understanding.  
The spiritual master is not produced by rubber-stamping an ordinary man.  
The spiritual master is faithful to Kṛṣṇa's instructions.  
The spiritual master is seeing Kṛṣṇa everywhere.  
The spiritual master is approached with submissive inquiry.  
The spiritual master is never says he is God.  
The spiritual master is free from all endeavors for mundane achievement.  
The spiritual master is never to be considered as bearing some material designation.  
The spiritual master is never to be criticized on the basis of physical boldly features.  
The spiritual master is deemed a success by making one disciple a pure devotee.  
The spiritual master is the transparent via medium to Kṛṣṇa.  
The spiritual master is the eternal father.  
The spiritual master is the external manifestation of Supersoul.  
The spiritual master is more kind than the Lord because he knows the mission of the Lord.  
The spiritual master is an ocean of auspicious qualities.  
The spiritual master is the deliverer of most fallen.  
The spiritual master is the transcendental moon holding back the tide of Kali-yuga.  
The spiritual master is mercy and love personified.  
The spiritual master is the friend of the poor.  
The spiritual master is the life and soul of the disciple.  
The spiritual master is remembered within the heart of the Lord.  
The spiritual master is ever fresh, like Kṛṣṇa.

Although these qualities are clearly visible, we fallen souls are too much perplexed by the workings of the material energy to appreciate such precious association. Please forgive our degraded condition.

## Ann Arbor

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

As a dwarf reaching for the moon or a candle trying to find out the sun, due to our conditioned nature we can barely ascertain and appreciate your sublime and exalted position.

But one thing we can understand is that had you not so kindly appeared before us we would have only this repeated holocaust of broken dreams and empty aspirations known as the material world as our future.

You are wisdom personified, the embodiment of compassion. Your words are the only standard of truth. By contact with a bona fide spiritual master one should come to know all things, both phenomenal and noumenal, and when listening to you this actually becomes a fact.

You have said that the path of *bhakti* is like nectar and it holds whatever one desires, and the way to take it is simply to follow. *Karmīs* say "dogs run free so why can't we?" but you have pointed out that even enjoyable dog's life depends on a good master. Everyone is serving something or someone, but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the best master. Please benedict us that we may eternally follow and serve you faithfully.

*Your servants in Ann Arbor,*

## Atlanta

O glorious master,  
all praises to thee  
You give eyes to the blind  
and melt hearts of steel.

We were in Kali's deep well  
given up hope  
But kindness has come in person  
you've passed us the rope.

O follower of the Gosvāmīs.  
You are their mercy-bearer  
One moment's touch with thee  
is the most precious elixer  
(the nectar of immortality).

Habituated to the false,  
we are addicts of poison  
As servants of lust  
we are bereft of good reason.

Therefore we must now beg  
as drowning men cry  
Please give us your lamp  
guide us through the night.

All souls are wandering  
aimless and amiss  
This rare life squandering  
hell's in our midst.

The jewel of human life  
lies in the camp of jackals  
But as a lion you strike  
reclaim the booty for Kṛṣṇa.

Your Hanumān-like challenge  
rings everywhere true and bold  
All pious men awaken  
and at your lotus feet take hold.

As at Kurukṣetra's destined battle  
Kṛṣṇa's foes did quake  
So the false bottom of science  
You have given a fatal shake.

Let every fool or sage  
follow your guiding glances  
The world you can engage  
in devotional service, the matchless gift.

The measure of your love  
in eternity can't be found  
Yet such selfless pure love  
in your divine books is bound.

Atlanta ISKCON  
Tennesee Farm

## Auckland

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, the greatest of all  
having taken shelter at Kṛṣṇa's lotus feet  
you are our only master and guide  
to save us from māyā's defeat

Your ISKCON movement is a transcendental ship  
to carry us over the ocean of death  
and you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the captain of the trip  
giving us nectar with every breath

Every word you speak is so perfect  
every line you write so sublime  
every song you sing to glorify the Lord  
melts our hearts so hardened by Time

You have pierced through our covering of māyā  
we've been carrying since time immemorial  
vainly searching for happiness in this material world  
through sense enjoyment so ephemeral

By your divine instructions we've come to know  
of a higher goal in this human birth  
than just eating, sleeping, mating and defending  
which is all animal life is worth

For so long we've to taken to devotional service  
not caring for the mercy of the Lord  
so therefore quite willingly we've drunk poison  
with suffering our only reward

Despite this misery we were still thinking  
"I am going to enjoy one day"  
just like an ass so foolish and stubborn  
we struggled to enjoy in this way

Always misled by our fickle minds  
and our senses which never learn  
that material enjoyment just increases frustration  
like fuel helps a fire to burn

Not only were we cheated by our body and mind  
but by our so-called leaders as well  
bogus yogīs, politicians and religious men  
who themselves have nothing to tell

about actually what is the goal of life  
nor the process how to gain it  
they may have their various formulas for peace  
but where do we see them attain it?

And so misguided we've followed their whims  
controlled by māyā's demand  
But now you have come to save us dear Prabhupāda  
and lead us to Kṛṣṇa's command

We owe you our very life and soul  
everything we have is yours  
your mercy is all we are made of  
and to serve you always is the perfection  
of our lives. Jaya Gurudeva!!

## Barcelona

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

The *mṛdaṅga* sounds all over the world. In every town big or small the devotees are singing the holy names. In the ear of every conditioned soul this transcendental vibration is penetrating. The prediction of Lord Caitanya is being accomplished, as well as the desire of your spiritual master.

In the middle of this dense and permanent darkness of Kali-yuga, when everybody was completely sleeping, you came like a true father. Conscious of the danger, you made us awaken from our horrible nightmare.

*jīva jāgo, jīva jāgo, gorācaṅda bole*  
*kota nidrā jāo māyā-pīśāccīra kole*

**"Wake up sleeping souls!**  
**Wake up, sleeping souls!**  
**How long will you sleep on the lap**  
**of the witch called māyā?"**

You have awakened us and continuously you are giving us the mercy of your instructions. But because of our bad habits we do not carry them out correctly. We are only an obstacle making your mission difficult.

How to thank you enough for what you are doing for us? To be humble, you have explained to us, one must be humble. But the example is your example: "I cannot be God, for I truly am small, but He kindly allows me to serve Him, that's all."

In this material world, people are trying to form different groups, political societies, religions, etc., without being conscious that in this way they are strengthening the chains that are already imprisoning them.

You have been our true liberator, because you are "Jagad-guru—*guru* of the universe—bringing us the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa as it is:

*sarva-dharmān parityajya*  
*mām ekaṁ śaraṇam vraja*  
*ahaṁ tvāṁ sarva-pāpebhyo*  
*mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ*

**"Abandon all varieties of religion and just surrender unto Me. I shall deliver you from all sinful reaction. Do not fear." \[Bg. 18.66\]**

And you said "That is His mercy transcendental and sweet. Take it... be happy!

Always bestowing your unlimited mercy, you are giving humanity the biggest reservoir of love in your incomparable translations of Vedic literature.

Thus, we only ask the space to be an atom at your lotus feet and the opportunity to serve you eternally.

*jaya oṁ viṣṇu-pāda paramahaṁsa*  
*parivrājakācārya aṣṭottara-śata śrī śrimad*  
*bhaktivedānta gosvāmī mahārāja*  
*Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya*

**"One hundred and eight glories to the wandering mendicant, to the great** ***bhakta*** **who resembles a swan, to the glorious master Śrī Śrimad Bhaktivedānta Swami Mahārāja Prabhupāda, who has taken refuge at the lotuslike feet of Viṣṇu."**

## BBT Library Party

Let us offer our most humble obeisances again and again at the lotus feet of our spiritual master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

In the *Śrīmad Bhāgavatam,* Vidura appropriately addresses his spiritual master, Maitreya Muni, thusly:

*janasya kṛṣṇād vimukhasya daivād*  
*adharma śilasya suduḥkhitasya*  
*anugrahāyeha caranti nūnaṁ*  
*bhūtāni bhavyāni janārdanasya*

**"O my Lord, great philanthropic souls travel on the earth on behalf of the Supreme Personality of Godhead to show compassion to the fallen souls, who are adverse to the sense of subordination to the Lord." (*****Bhāg.*** **3.5.3)**

There is no more fitting glorification of Your Divine Grace than the very *Bhāgavatam* which you yourself personify. We, unfortunately, can understand very little of this fact, being of this class of fallen souls adverse to Kṛṣṇa *bhakti,* but by your causeless mercy we are becoming more and more appreciative of your greatness through the pages of the transcendental literatures which you are so kindly making available to the world at large. That is your magnanimity, that you are personally traveling to every town and village in the form of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and entering the homes, lives and hearts of all that you meet, thus creating the most wonderful revolution. *Pratinivṛtta guṇormi:* such materially conditioned souls as ourselves become gradually abhorred by our sinful lives and somehow or other become attracted to the devotional service of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the very fact that you are producing so many volumes of Kṛṣṇa conscious literature is the final proof for fools such as ourselves that you are not an ordinary entity of this material world. As Lord Brahmā became qualified to offer countless prayers in glorification of the Lord by dint of his being acquainted with the ocean of truth, so you are always cognizant of the name, form, qualities, activities and pastimes of Lord Kṛṣṇa and therefore are an endless river of nectar extending from the ocean of Kṛṣṇa to our parched hearts. The *gopīs* prayed to the Lord in this regard:

*tava kathāmṛtaṁ tapta-jīvanaṁ*  
*kavibhir īḍitaṁ kalmaṣapaham*  
*śravaṇa maṅgalaṁ śrīmad-ātataṁ*  
*bhuvi gṛṇanti te bhūridā janāḥ*

**"My Lord, the nectar of Your words and the description of your activities are the life and soul of those who are always aggrieved in this material world. Those narrations are transmitted by exalted personalities and they eradicate all sinful reaction. Whoever hears these narrations attains all good fortune. These narrations are broadcast all over the world and are filled with spiritual power. Those who spread the message of Godhead are certainly the most munificent welfare workers." (*****Bhāg.*** **10.31.9)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we, your Western disciples, stand as evidence of the immense potency of your words. Although we come from lower than *mleccas* and *yavanas,* by your order we are in a tiny way assisting you in your mission to save the world. This, again, is simply by your causeless mercy, and is further evidence of your equanimity toward all living entities, in that you are saving us by engaging us in saving others. And yet you are so kind upon us to see that we get credit in Kṛṣṇa's eyes for our feeble efforts in pushing on this *saṅkīrtana* movement. Although this is bewildering to us, how fallen rascals such as ourselves can be counted among the authorized representatives of Lord Caitanya, it is so by Your Divine Grace, and it is therefore not at all astonishing. As stated in the *Garuḍa Purāṇa:*

*kīṭa-pakṣi-mṛgāṇāṁ ca*  
*harau sannyasta-karmaṇām*  
*ūrdhvam eva gatiṁ manye*  
*kiṁ punar jñānināṁ nṛṇām*

**"Even the worms, birds, and beasts are assured of elevation to the highest perfection of life if they are completely surrendered to the transcendental loving service of the Lord, so what to speak of the philosophers among human beings?"**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by Your Divine Grace we who were certainly lowborn creatures are now distributing your mercy in the form of your books to the learned philosophers, scholars, professors and theologians of the Western world. The fact that we of the BBT Library Party are able to do this work and that the response of the learned men of this materialistic society toward your books is so overwhelmingly favorable, is only further evidence of your expertise. You exactly fit Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī's definition of an *ācārya,* in that you are capable of bringing everyone, somehow or other, to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please favor us by allowing us to always remember your position as the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. We pray at your lotus feet that we never make the mistake of minimizing any instruction that you may impart as being subject to the defects of this material world. let us always perceive you as the person *Bhāgavatam,* the personified instructions of Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself. Please teach us to cherish the version of the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta:*

*guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.45).**

## Berkeley

In Kāśi Miśra's house at Purī one old man chants from six to six. Because of his old body he sometimes gets the words wrong, or leaves them out, or forgets of bang the *karatālas,* or dozes off to sleep.

When the soldiers of time make their final assaults on the city of nine gates, it will be very difficult to remember Kṛṣṇa. Mahaṛajā Kulaśekhāra thought that it would be easier to remember Kṛṣṇa if he died early, and he prayed for it.

Service to the spiritual master is the boat to cross the ocean of birth and death. An ignorant farm boy sometimes becomes a national hero in an emergency. In kali-yuga, anyone, whether he be woman, merchant or low class can approach the supreme destination by service to the Lord and His representative. We thank our spiritual master for giving us the nine items of devotional service. If diligently pursued in life, they well harbor in our thoughts at death.

A worm becomes a butterfly due to psychological changes in the mind. Similarly, services to the spiritual master are the wings whereby undisciplined, low-born worms like ourselves swiftly ascend to the platform of eternal life, bliss and knowledge. Let us take that service as our life and soul and always be happy.

*Berkeley Yatra*

## Bombay

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda:*

*bhavantam evānucaran nirantaraṁ*  
*praśānta-niḥśeṣa-mano-rathāntaraḥ*  
*kadāham aikāntika-nitya-kṅkaraḥ*  
*praharṣayiṣyāmi sa-nātha-jīvitaḥ*  
*(Stotra-ratnam 46)*

By serving you constantly, one is freed from all material desires and is completely pacified. When shall we engage as your permanent eternal servants and always feel joyful to have such a perfect master?

The living entities are fragmental parts of the Supreme Lord, and they are eternal. But due to conditional life they are struggling very hard with the six senses, which include the mind.

*mamaivāṁśo jīva-loke*  
*jīva-bhūtaḥ sanātanaḥ*  
*manaḥ ṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇi*  
*prakṛti-sthāni karṣati*  
*(Bg. 15.7)*

The mind is described as *mano-ratha* because the mind acts as a *ratha* or chariot, driving the living entity from place to place in pursuit of the fulfillment of his desires. The mind is the center of the senses and receives input from the senses, which are demanding gratification in terms of the objects of the senses. The mind is always busy accepting and rejecting on the basis of information received from the senses. Thus our mental chariot carries us from place to place, universe to universe, body after body, millennium after millennium in the vain pursuit of illusory material happiness.

*śarīraṁ yad avāpnoti*  
*yac cāpy utkrāmatiśvaraḥ*  
*gṛhītvaitāni saṁyāti*  
*vāyur gandhān ivāśayāt*  
*(Bg. 15.8)*

The living entity in the material world carries his different conceptions of life from one body to another, as the air carries aromas. Thus he takes one kind of body and again quits it to take another.

According to our mental conception at the time of death, we take on another body made of various senses, and we enjoy and suffer according to the senses we develop.

*śrotraṁ caksuḥ sparśanaṁ ca*  
*rasanam ghrāṇam eva ca*  
*adhiṣṭhāya manaś cāyaṁ*  
*viṣayān upasevate*  
*(Bg. 15.9)*

The living entity, thus taking another gross body, obtains a certain type of ear, tongue, and nose and sense of touch, which are grouped about the mind. He thus enjoys a particular set of sense objects.

But despite wanderings from body to body, universe to universe, our senses are never satisfied, and our mind is never pacified.

In order to relieve the suffering of the conditioned souls wandering throughout the material worlds, the Lord advises in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (18.66):

*sarva-dharmān parityajya*  
*mām ekaṁ śaranaṁ vraja*  
*ahaṁ tvāṁ sarva-pāpebhyo*  
*mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ*

Abandon all engagements and just surrender unto Me. I shall deliver you from all sinful reactions. Do not fear.

We are receiving so many dictations from the mind and senses; we have to reject those dictations and accept the dictations of Kṛṣṇa and His representative. We have manufactured so many engagements and duties, but the basic principle is sense gratification, and sense gratification can never satisfy the soul. The tongue is dictating, the belly is dictating, the genitals are dictating, all of the senses are dictating: "I want to enjoy." But instead of enjoying, we are suffering. We are not enjoyers. We are meant to serve Kṛṣṇa. Thus the mind is constantly agitated, falsely trying to enjoy, accepting and rejecting, never caring to remember the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa or His representative. Therefore the Lord advises us to give up all of our materialistic plan-making and just surrender to His plan. The basic desire for sense gratification expands into so many "isms," such as communism, socialism, humanitarianism, altruism and philanthropism, but still the basic principle is sense gratification, limited or extended. Therefore the Lord advises us to give up all these "isms" and just take to Vaiṣṇavism—service to Viṣṇu or His representative. When the living entity surrenders to the Lord and realizes his constitutional position as the eternal servant of the Lord, he becomes peaceful and acts of the joyful platform as a liberated soul.

*bhoktāraṁ yajña-tapasāṁ*  
*sarva-loka-maheśvaram*  
*suhṛdaṁ sarva-bhūtānāṁ*  
*jñṇtvā māṁ śāntim ṛccati*  
*(Bg. 5.29)*

**"The sages knowing Me as the ultimate purpose of all sacrifices and austerities, the Supreme Lord of all planets and demigods and the benefactor and well-wisher of all living entities, attain peace from the pangs of material miseries."**

After all, we have to serve someone or something, but the perfection of our service is to serve you. We have been serving our bodies and our minds, inventing so many *dharmas* or isms to serve, serving anything and everything except your lotus feet. But we now want to kick out all other dictations and surrender to the dictations of your lotus feet. By accepting you as our perfect master we become peaceful and joyful, free from material desires, *praśānta.*

A stray dog wanders here and there, miserable and filled with anxiety. He does not know where to find food or rest. Sometimes he roams from place to place searching for some scraps to eat, but he finds nothing. Sometimes he takes rest along side of the road, only to be disturbed by cruel kicks and harsh words. His body is lean and ragged; his mind is morose and helpless. Sometimes big dogs snarl and bite him or little children throw stones and chase him. But he is alone, hopeless; no master, *anātha.*

A dog with a master is happy. He is fat and content. He wags his tail and barks loudly. He is confident. No one can bother him, because he has his master. He knows food will come. He has his place to stay. In all circumstances he knows his master will protect him. Thus he is happy, *sanātha.*

We pray to remain eternally as your dog. All we want in life is your service. We require a master, and in you we have found the perfect master. Without you we are lost, hopeless; with you we are jubilant, pacified. You alone can protect us, provide for us, and give us shelter. You are so kind that you have accepted hundreds and thousands of stray dogs and are leading us with you in a joyful procession. Animals may snarl and children may throw stones but we are always cheerful and confident because we have our master, Your Divine Grace. We pray to tag along at your lotus feet, eternally following you wherever you go. You may lead us to heaven of you may lead us to hell—we do not care. We want you and you only. Having found our master we have nothing more to desire. We are happy because we have you, the perfect master. We are *sanātha,* and have attained the goal of our life.

*Your eternal servants,* ISKCON Bombay

## Boston

Within this *brahmāṇḍa* or material sky there are billions of universes floating from the pores of Śrī Mahā-Viṣṇu. Within each universe there are, again, billions of planets containing varied atmospheres and gradations of living conditions. These planets provide accommodating space for the limitless living entities who, having forgotten their original relationship with Kṛṣṇa, are wandering from one situation to another through 8,400,000 species of life, in search of happiness. Filling up the entire *brahmāṇḍa* with life, these living entities, described as measuring one ten-thousandth to top of a tip of a hair, can be divided into two groups, the nonmoving (plants, trees and stones) and the moving (birds, aquatics, and animals). Among all of these billions of living entities only a very small portion are said to be human beings. And among those who are considered human, most fall into the category of the uncivilized, due to their aversion or ignorance of the codes governing religious life enunciated in the *Vedas.* We are then left with a very small remaining portion of human beings who are recognized as civilized because of their adherence to the Vedic principles. Of these, almost one-half are simply following in a superficial way, while committing all kinds of sinful activities. Among the half who are truly following the *Vedas,* most are interested in the *karma-kāṇḍa* section and are aspiring to enjoy the results of their good works and pious activities. Out of many thousands of such sincere fruitive workers, perhaps one becomes wise and realizes the futility of his endeavor. Out of many millions of such wise men one may actually become liberated from the karmic cycle. And out of many millions of such liberated souls, who compromise the minutest portion of the total aggregate of living beings existing within this material world, a pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa is very difficult to find. Therefore, on this most auspicious day of the appearance of our spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇu-pāda Paramahaṁsa Paravrājakācārya Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is impossible for us to properly express our gratitude or fathom our unique fortune that out of all the trillions of uncountable creatures in existence, somehow or other, we have been blessed by the holy association of the living representative of Śrīla Vyāsadeva, the original compiler of all transcendental knowledge, coming down in the preceptorial line originating with Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Himself. Such an authorized spiritual master is the rarest, most valuable gem to be found in all the three worlds. It is only by the brilliance of his flawless desire to act solely for the satisfaction of Lord Kṛṣṇa that the entire human civilization can be delivered from all anxieties arising from the frustration of lusty desires.

Within the hearts of the conditioned souls there are hundreds and thousands of lusty desires for enjoyment in this material world. Being driven by these desires they are taking one body after another in an attempt to achieve satisfaction. However, because of the very nature of this material world, which Kṛṣṇa declares to be *duḥkhālayam aśāśvatam,* a temporary place, full of miseries, the stage of satisfaction is never reached. They are thus becoming disappointed and fearful In the end death comes and they are forced to accept another material body, tightly bound up in a vast network of unlimited anxieties.

Sometimes one such illusioned living being foolishly approaches some so-called great personality, who has become expert in the fields of *karma, jñāna,* or *yoga,* to get some kind of relief. However, because such men have no knowledge of the eternal spiritual identity beyond this temporary body, and are bereft of a relationship with God, their instructions do not give him ultimate satisfaction. We have practical experience of this in the modern world. At present, the major portion of the human energy has been expended on vicious works with the aim of achieving a higher grade of sense gratification. This we have done. We have also invented innumerable philosophies and so-called "paths" by which we are trying to enhance our human experience. Yet, people are wondering why there is so much unhappiness and disunity and they have created many different "isms" in an attempt to deal with the situation. Unfortunately all such "isms" have helped only to intensify and not to diminish the problems of life. The leaders of the world, being blind to the knowledge of the self, can only give symptomatic treatment and cannot understand the root cause of the dissatisfaction which plagues all of us. That was described by Rāmānanda Rāya to Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu. Lord Caitanya asked Rāmānanda Rāya: "Of all kinds of distress, what is the most painful?" Rāmānanda Rāya replied, "Apart from separation from the devotees of Kṛṣṇa I know of no other unbearable unhappiness." In the light of this statement, we can understand that the present dissatisfaction experienced on all levels of our present social structure is not due to the government, crime, politics, economics, men, women, etc. Neither will it be mitigated by artificial methods for the so-called improvement of our living conditions. The real fact is that as long as any civilization or society is bereft of the association of the most confidential servitor of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Gurudeva, there can never be even a moment of true happiness for anyone. We therefore long for the day when the bona fide *ācārya,* Śrīla Prabhupāda, will be recognized and sought after by the present world leaders for the absolute guidance required in order to progress. According to science's law of thermodynamics, a complex system, if left to itself, tends towards chaos and disintegration. Similarly any world organization which neglects the absolute direction of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the *Vedas* and the bona fide spiritual master will ultimately destroy itself. Modern day philosophers, politicians, social workers and all other theoreticians must give up their limited empiric endeavors which will never turn back the rising tide of degradation, either individually or collectively, and surrender, at once, to the lotus feet of the Guru. The spiritual master brings with him the won ingredient which, when added, will make the human endeavor meaningful and ultimately successful. That ingredient is devotional service to the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa. In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* this is explained: "The supreme occupation (*dharma*) for all humanity is that by which men can attain to loving devotional service unto the transcendent Lord. Such devotional service must be unmotivated and uninterrupted in order to completely satisfy the self." In another place it is declared, "Anyone whose work is not meant to elevate him to religious life, anyone whose religious ritualistic performances do not raise him to renunciation and anyone situated in renunciation that does not lead him to devotional service to the Supreme Lord must be considered dead although he is breathing." We therefore offer millions of obeisances unto the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda, whose glorious appearance in this dead world is giving all living beings new hope for life. All glories, all glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, the Supreme Personality of servitor Godhead, who is as worshipable as Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself! In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi* 1.45) it is declared, "According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees." Millions of obeisances again and again to Śrīla Prabhupāda, who is an ocean of compassion upon the fallen souls of the Western world. He is always situated in spiritual happiness, yet he also experiences unhappiness. He is unhappy to see the sufferings of others. Therefore, he is benedicting the entire world with his causeless mercy and delivering all living beings.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our humble obeisances. We pray unto your lotus feet to please bestow upon us just one drop of your mercy, so that we may have no desire within our heart that is apart from yours. Purify us of all traces of self-motivation. Then, accepting your order as our life and soul, perhaps we may be of some small assistance in your tireless endeavor to shower the cooling moonlike rays of the lotus feet of Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda upon the suffering humanity. This alone will bring about ultimate relief of all miseries brought about by our "causeless unwillingness to serve and love the Supreme."

Śrīla Prabhupāda, "Kṛṣṇa is yours. You have the power to give Him to me. I am simply running behind you shouting, 'Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa'"

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!!

The devotees of Boston ISKCON

## Brazil

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Our beloved spiritual master Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet, which are as refreshing as the light of thousands of moons, and which are our only refuge from the constant attacks that we suffer at the hands of the powerful energy of *māyā.*

Oh Śrīla Prabhupāda, we were always entangled in the material energy of *mahā-māyā.* We were always engaged in the lowest forms of sense gratification. We were afflicted and tormented, being without protection and shelter. But you, O Jagad-guru, came to answer our cry, dedicating your life to saving us suffering, fallen souls, and thus giving up the peaceful atmosphere of the holy places like Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur. You came to the hellish Western countries, full of voidism and impersonalism, thus satisfying the desire if Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and the order of your beloved spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī said that one who has desires to enjoy material things should never look at the young face of Govinda, for we know, beloved master, that it would be then impossible to go with material life. Here we had experienced in Brazil a little bit of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, but suddenly everything was finished and we began to suffer separation.

Thus, in this painful condition, we begged for your help. We asked that you extend your hand to pull us out of the mire of material life. Immediately by your causeless mercy, you came and saved us. You saved us from drowning in illusion, from the physical relations of the material world. You saved us from forgetting Kṛṣṇa. How, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can we show our gratitude? What do we have to give you, except what you have given to us? Since we are nothing, and we have nothing, we only beg you that you accept our body, words and mind in your service, and that you use us like puppets in your mission to satisfy Kṛṣṇa through the *saṅkīrtana-yajña.* We are begging, Śrīla Prabhupāda, only that you never stop engaging us in the service of the Lord, and that you permit that even in spite of our constant offenses that we go on purifying ourselves on the service of your lotus feet. Where else can we find shelter, and what else can we do except serve Your Divine Grace?

*śrī-guru karuṇā sindhu, adhama janāra bandhu*  
*lokanāth lokera jīvana*  
*hā hā prabhu kara dayā, deha more pada-chāyā*  
*ebe yāśa ghuṣuka tribhuvana*

**"Our spiritual master is the ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor, and the lord and master of the devotees. O master! Be merciful unto me. Give me the shade of your lotus feet. Your fame is spread all over the three worlds."**

This immense country is completely in the darkness of ignorance, its thousands and thousands of persons simply sinking in nescience. We have the fervent desire to show these people that you are the *ācārya,* the pure devotee of the Lord. We want to show them that you have come here within the *mahat-tattva* only for the purpose of giving them *kṛṣṇa-prema,* but without your mercy, our endeavors to show them will be all useless.

*yasya prasādād bhagavata-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*  
*dhyāyaṁ stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"By the mercy of the spiritual master, one is benedicted with the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, no one can make any spiritual advancement. Therefore I should always remember my spiritual master. At least three times a day I should offer my most respectful obeisances at the lotus feet of my spiritual master."**

We implore you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, prostrated with our heads in the dust of your feet, for your blessings and your mercy. O beloved Guru, this entire world will be reconquered for its original owner, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You have explained to us that the material energy is a product of the spiritual energy. Therefore, the whole world can be turned into Vaikuṇṭha, if we have your mercy. Therefore, we will go on fighting hard, waiting for the day when, by your grace, we return to transcendental bliss.

## Bridesville

We, the devotees of New Gokula dhāma, offer our prostrate obeisances at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are the greatest of all mystics, for you are performing the greatest miracle. You are taking Westerners from the dark pit of material sense gratification and turning them into twice-born *brāhmaṇas.* Although we were desiring to remain in the mire of material existence, by your desires and prayers the Supreme Lord has acted from within our hearts to inspire us to take to devotional service.

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde paya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**"According to their** ***karma,*** **all living entities are wandering throughout the universes. Some of them are being elevated to the upper planetary systems and some are going to the lower ones. Out of many millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets an opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. By the mercy of both Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, such a person receives the seed of devotional service." (Cc.** ***Madhya*** **19.151)**

We were drowning in the waves and whirlpools of the modes of material nature, rushing towards death and repeated birth, not even realizing it. You have saved us from that hellish ocean, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can only offer our prayers of gratitude. It is said that when a pure devotee narrates the unadulterated narrations of the Lord's transcendental pastimes, that narration is compared to a raging river. Even a casual bystander who happens to hear a pure devotee's speech is swept into that river until he eventually reaches the ocean of devotional service. The *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* states, "The value of one moment's association with a pure devotee of the Lord cannot even compare to the attainment of heavenly planets, liberation from matter, or attainment of worldly prosperity which is meant for death."

We have somehow received this causeless grace by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Therefore, in gratitude we wish to dedicate our lives to fulfilling your desires.

How can we ever repay you, Gurudeva, for you are giving us *rāga vidyā, rāja guhyam,* the king of knowledge and education; *padaṁ gachanty anāmāyām,* that state beyond all miseries; *śāśvataṁ padam avyayam,* the eternal and imperishable abode; and *śāntim apnoti naiṣṭhikīva,* unadulterated peace.

You are the most munificent person for you are directly empowered by Lord Kṛṣṇa, as confirmed by Uddhava:

*naivopanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo 'ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aṣbhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caitya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**"O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features, externally as the** ***ācārya*** **and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You." (*****Bhāg.*** **11.29.6)**

Liberation from this material world is possible by knowing the transcendental nature of the pastimes and activities of the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa. We Westerners are so fallen that we did not even have a taste for hearing those auspicious narrations. But you are so kind, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are blessing us with this affinity for hearing. This is explained in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* by Sūta Gosvāmī:

*śuśrūṣoh śraddadhānasya*  
*vāsudeva-kathā-ruciḥ*  
*syān mahat-sevayā viprāḥ*  
*puṇya-tīrtha-niṣevaṇāt*

**"O twice-born sages, by serving those devotees who are completely freed from all vice, great service is done. By such service, one gains an affinity for hearing the messages of Vāsudeva." (*****Bhāg*** **1.2.16)**

Human reasoning and logic cannot understand this miracle, for it is causeless mercy and nothing else.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our happiness, gratitude and love for you forces us to dance in ecstasy when we hear your name being chanted, like Śrutadeva when he saw Lord Kṛṣṇa. You are purifying our contaminated hearts and giving us the ultimate goal of life, love of God. Your mercy is so sublime that by your grace, we can easily cross the ocean of material miseries. The sublime potency of devotional service and the spiritual master is nicely stated by Sūta Gosvāmī in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam:*

*naṣṭa-prāyeṣv abhadreṣu*  
*nityaṁ bhāgavata-sevayā*  
*bhagavaty uttama-śloke*  
*bhaktir bhavati naiṣṭhikī*

**"By regularly hearing the** ***Bhāgavatam*** **and rendering service unto the pure devotee, all that is troublesome to the heart is practically destroyed, and loving service unto the glorious Lord, who is praised with transcendental songs, is established as an irrevocable fact." (*****Bhāg,*** **1.2.18)**

You have given us the supreme *dharma* or occupation. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Here at New Gokula you have taught us how to live peacefully and simply, protecting the cows, growing grains and vegetables, hearing *bhāgavata-dharma* discourses and worshipping our deities, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana. We pray that our endeavors here will justly present the Kṛṣṇa conscious lifestyle to the vision of the Western world. Our hope is that all mankind will give up its suffering and toil and take to this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, thus fulfilling your desire, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to terminate forever the suffering of mankind.

New Gokula, Bridesville

## Buenos Aires

*Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, O ocean of mercy, may the swan of our mind enter the flower of your lotus feet and become locked up in the network of your transcendental qualities. In this way our hearts will become cleansed of all material affection.

You are the cause of all pleasure for the sincere disciple willing to follow the footprints left behind by Your Divine Grace. Your pearl-like tears of love flow down and mix with the holy dust which you touch. You show the way back home. Let us follow you birth after birth.

Like a golden parrot you touch that which is already sweet, turning it into nectar so delicious. You uplift even one so fallen as I who do not know the glories of your feet, which can give shelter to all because you are the most dear servant of Hari, Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Being.

You are the only one who can polish the rough diamonds of our hearts and present them as an offering to the lover of the *gopīs,* the darling of Yaśodā, and the cause of all that be. We pray that He may give us unflinching loving service unto your lotus feet.

*Your most fallen servants:*

Pañcadravida Svāmī  
All the assembled devotees of the Temple of Buenos Aires

## Buffalo

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories unto you. I am a materialistic rascal and have no good qualifications but somehow or other I have gained shelter at your lotus feet.

*A voice,*  
Strewn with saffron mercy particles  
from the lotus feet of the Lord,  
Resounded in our material night,  
With sounds of jīva jāga, until then  
perfection was no where in sight;  
But rather than lines of poesy  
about the pure devotee glories,  
the Vedas have already told,  
Better a life of serving,  
the message you have brought,  
Like an animal sold.

We beg to remain in your service.

## Caracas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Father:*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. You are liberating all fallen souls, causing the vibration of the holy names of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare, Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare to be heard in every corner of the creation. Devoid of your shelter we are like dry trees without leaves or flowers, and your kind presence enables us to live in the garden of Vṛndāvana, where the plaints of unalloyed love for Kṛṣṇa grow.

Now that your devotional magnificence is gracing the world, and Lord Caitanya's mercy bathes all living beings, we want to chant about your love for Kṛṣṇa. Our eyes cannot see that love, but our desire is to become infected with this love, and we know that by following you we will certainly obtain it.

You are doing the greatest benefit to human society and you are the perfect captain of the boat that can take us all to the permanent shelter of Kṛṣṇa's lotus feet.

We are all made of your mercy, and all transcendental knowledge comes only from you, since you are the authentic representative of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Any spiritual strength and any possibility of renunciation also come from you. We had lived like animals but now you are giving us the chance of reaching the supreme goal of life: pure love for Kṛṣṇa.

Just like soft lotus petals, your divine feet have marked all over the world a path of victory, truth, honesty and purity.

You show everyone that it is possible to live a perfect life. We do not have any way of repaying you.

Please let us serve you eternally and kindly accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*Sus sirvientes eternos,*

## Carriere

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to the appearance day of 108 Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda! This most blessed event, Vyāsa-Pūjā, gives us the opportunity to offer our feeble glorifications and gratitude to your spiritual master. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our appreciation can never equal the value of your mercy toward us. To get a bona fide spiritual master is the most rare and fortunate thing in the material world. Out of the millions of conditioned souls wandering in the material world, only a few will meet a pure devotee, by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. Then, by the mercy of a pure devotee like yourself, the conditioned souls like us get the chance to meet Kṛṣṇa—face to face.

*Yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*

Actually, we can understand, without very much examination, that we are not qualified either for your mercy or for success in spiritual life. We are envious offenders, always lazy and neglectful of your instruction. Out of three ways to attain perfection, we are only making advancement by your mercy (*kṛpā-siddha,* the mercy of a Vaiṣṇava). One can attain perfection by always remembering Kṛṣṇa (*nitya-siddha*), but we only remember Kṛṣṇa because you have reminded us of Him. One can also attain perfection by following the rules and regulations of devotional service (*sādhana-siddha*), but it is by your potency, protection, and instruction that we follow any rules or regulations at all. Factually, your mercy is all we are made of.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have told us what you want us to do so many times in your books. You have told us to chant, dance, follow the regulative principles, and especially to preach Lord Caitanya's movement all over the world, and by these activities become successful in spiritual life. We will be able to preach and become successful only if we have complete faith in what you have taught us. You have already set the perfect example. This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is going all over the world simply because you had implicit faith in the words of your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. Faith in your words is the secret to all success in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**"Only unto those great souls who have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of Vedic knowledge automatically revealed." \[ŚU 6.23\]**

So, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our preaching work here on your farm in Mississippi has taken the forms of setting up a spiritual community as an example for others to follow, building facilities for people to come and live with Kṛṣṇa at the center of their lives, growing fruits and vegetables and protecting Kṛṣṇa's blissful, milk-giving cows. So, we have tried to show a little gratitude with words for the opportunity you are giving us, now we pray to spend our lives glorifying you with our works. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*The devotees of the Hare Kṛṣṇa farm, Carriere, Mississippi*

## Charlotte

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Thank you Śrīla Prabhupāda for coming to this country and saving us. Your kindness is a fathomless ocean, your compassion knows no bounds. Indeed your fame is spread all over the three worlds. Thank you for allowing us to engage in your service. By developing attachment to you, our attachment to Kṛṣṇa is guaranteed. You are most dear to Kṛṣṇa, therefore you are very much like Kṛṣṇa, completely pure and full of blissfulness. By approaching you in the proper way, our mental boundaries are broken and our bodily limitations are reduced almost to nil; thus our conditional life comes to an end. Just by remembering you, the most difficult thing becomes very easy. And when we forget you the easiest thing becomes very difficult.

My prayer to you is to always be able to think of you in all my activities, otherwise they are simply a waste of time. This human life is so short and the mind is so flickering; please accept this prayer so that the valuable time will not pass in vain.

May we ever remain indebted to your service, as long as you will have us.

All glories to your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your fallen disciples at ISKCON's Charlotte outpost.*

## Chicago

By the grace of our spiritual master, our lives have become factually auspicious. The unsurpassable degree of auspiciousness with which our lives have become filled is only scantily perceived and less still remembered by our fallen selves.

Wandering upwards to the higher planetary systems and again down to the lower, we have tasted the false *amṛta* of materialistic life. It is a dancing mirage the witch *māyā* has drawn us into the depths of her desert of illusion, flying as bewildered moths into blazing fire. Superficial, impotent philosophies impelled us after *māyā's* beckoning mirage—to enjoy as lords of this material nature. False family, friendship and society have claimed to serve us; encouraging us (only for a price) to continue to death our quest for the unattainable illusion. This impure motive of lust has polluted our hearts and ignorance has perpetuated our insane pursuit after the mirage of happiness in this barren desert of material life.

It is you alone Śrīla Prabhupāda who can save us, you alone who can remove this ignorance:

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñanāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śri-gurave namaḥ*

**"I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge."**

Everyone in the material world is trapped, bound by the insurpassable power of *māyā.* Not the greatest scholar, not the strongest man, the wealthiest aristocrat, most beautiful woman, nor most renounced *yogī* can escape her powerful grip. It is only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who can give us the mercy of Lord Kṛṣṇa, to take us from *māyā's* control.

*ei-bāra-karuṇā kara vaiṣṇava-gosāi*  
*patita-p-avana tomā vine keha nāi*  
*Yāṅhāra nikaṭe gele pāpa dūre yāya*  
*emana dayāla prabhu kebā kothā pāya*

O Vaiṣṇava Gosvāmī, please be merciful to me this one time. You are the savior of the fallen; without you there is no one. Just by being in your presence, sins go far away. Where can anyone find such kindness

You are the mercy representation of the Supreme Lord, who has so kindly given to the reluctant unappreciative souls the highest benediction of the knowledge and realization to actually perceive the foolishness of material life. You have delivered us from the nonsense relative world of misery where pain, frustration and anxiety are accepted as pleasure. You have given us a glimpse, a genuine experience of our eternal life with the Supreme Lord, Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Please kindly accept our meager services.

*kṛṣṇa se tomāra, kṛṣṇa dite pāra*  
*tomāra śakati āche*  
*āmi ta' kāṅgāla, 'kṛṣṇa' 'kṛṣṇa' boli',*  
*dhāi tava pāche pāche*

Kṛṣṇa is yours; you have to power to give Him to me I am simply running behind you shouting "Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa!"

*Your servants at ISKCON Chicago*

## Cleveland

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

The poet Narottama dāsa has sung *"śrī-guru-caraṇe rati, ei sei uttama-gati."* Attachment to the lotus feet of one's guru is the perfection which fulfills all desires. How is it that we, who are expert only in sinning, have been given this chance to attain the summit of self-realization? This is your causeless mercy upon us. You have descended to display the potency of the Supreme Lord, whose absolute purity cleanses anyone in contacts. By your grace, you are traveling everywhere to benedict the most fallen souls of the Age of Iron. The results are apparent. Wherever your lotus feet have touched, devotees have come forth and temples have grown up. But for your good self, we find no one else so active, so successful in realizing the prophesy of Lord Caitanya.

Your mission has been given by Lord Gaurasundara Himself, who commanded that anyone who takes his birth in the land of Bhārata-varṣa is responsible for delivering this *saṅkīrtana* movement to the rest of the world. Now, you have come, as foretold by Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda, just to fulfill that promise.

All of us here are without knowledge of the *Vedas* and know nothing of austerity. Because of our low nature we are unfaithful to our vows and even after accepting initiation from you, we fail to surrender. But you are the epitome of patience. You never turn us away, despite our countless offenses, but over and over repeat instructions to us who are so slow to learn.

Most munificent spiritual master, please grant that all our mundane ambitions will become destroyed. In time, even our worthless selves will become purified by your mercy, if only we remain under the shelter of your instructions. Kindly save us from the unceasing onslaught of the witch *māyā,* who is constantly reminding us of our former ways and attempting to lure us back. Our memories are short, our minds are decayed and our senses are very strong, having had control over us for so long. But for your grace we have no hope at all, no possibility of rising above animal life. Our position is so fearful. If we had perished before, there would have been no great loss, because we were so fallen already. But now that we have caught sight of the supreme goal, now that we have tasted a drop of the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, now there is so much at stake. Please don't let *māyā* cheat us anymore. We want to become your disciples; we want to work for you because we have never before seen anyone capable of delivering so much good to so many men. We want to see the fulfillment of your mission because no one else has ever brought us such a vision of total happiness.

We are your eternal servants; our link with you is your instructions. In this way, keep us in contact with your lotus feet. Their touch alone can cure the disease we suffered from time immemorial. All glories to your unbounded mercy!

*The devotees of Cleveland ISKCON*

## Columbia

You were with Kṛṣṇa and He sent you,  
To a world of death, where chewing the chewed  
Is the goal, and gratifying the senses  
It the "Golden Rule."  
Now Śrīla Prabhupāda has descended,  
With Caitanya's mercy we are benedicted  
Ārātrika: I offer my body at your lotus feet,  
With incense and burning camphor sweet,  
With ghee lamp burning, I offer to your ankles my mind,  
And with scented rose water, I offer my words to your knees and thighs.  
I offer my life and soul with soft silk and flowers to your waist,  
Fan your chest, and wave the camara to your face.  
The taste of Goloka is in your smile,  
And in your books remnants of love,  
O SO, your mercy is all I'm made of.

## Costa Rica

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Our position is so precarious because although we are completely ashamed of our fallen condition we still grab for the stick of desire. We are always trying to become happy within this material world but this is like trying to be happy in a forest fire.

It was not possible to understand any of this before we came into your pure association, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Searching for someone with truth was like an old blind man asking some rouge for guidance and the rascal takes the opportunity to lead the blind man to an open well and then plunder him.

In such a world there was no one to trust until, somehow or other, we heard the nectar about Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa coming from your lotus mouth.

As Rūpa Gosvāmī has stated:

*ataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa nāmādi*  
*na bhaved grāhyam indriyaiḥ*

**"We cannot conceive the name, form and quality of Śrī Kṛṣṇa through our own material senses."**

Thus the only way it is possible to make any spiritual advancement along this path of knowledge and realization is by coming into contact with a pure devotee like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You are described as *niṣkiñcana*—one who has no material desire to enjoy this material world, but moreover you are *ātmārāma,* which means that you have no desire other than to engage in devotional service. Therefore our prime realization is:

*vinā mahat pāda-rājo 'bhiṣkam*

One cannot attain perfection without smearing the dust from the lotus feet of an exalted devotee on his head.

We pray to remain fixed in remembering our greatest fortune, which has been the appearance and activities of Your Divine Grace.

On this day, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your disciples of your Costa Rica Mandira beg that you always allow us to take shelter of your association and instructions. We offer our *daṇḍavats* at your lotus feet.

*The devotees at ISCKON Costa Rica*

## Dallas

*tad viddhi praṇipātena*  
*paripraśena sevayā*  
*upadekṣyanti te jñānaṁ*  
*jñāninas tattva-darśinaḥ*

**"Just try to learn the truth by approaching a spiritual master. Inquire from him submissively and render service unto him. The self-realized soul can impart knowledge unto you because he has seen the truth." (Bg. 4.34)**

On this appearance day of our beloved spiritual master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta swami Prabhupāda, let us try to understand the actual position of the bona fide *ācārya* and the relationship he has with his disciples. Just as a man suffering from an illness seeks help from a qualified physician in order that he might be cured, so also an individual infected by the disease of material consciousness must seek the help of the self-realized spiritual master. One who does not seek out transcendental assistance faces the risk of spoiling his life.

"After all," they argue, "I am simply acting the way that I was taught by my parents and teachers. How can so many people be following a completely erroneous conception of life? Besides, many of us have sophisticated life styles, and we are highly trained and disciplined in various occupations. We are contributing members of our great society, and besides, I have my own religion. How can you accuse us of spoiling our lives?"

The answer is quite clearly given in the *śāstra: athāto brahma jijñāsā.* Now that we have this human form we must use it for solving the real problems of life. Simply by becoming polished animals is not the true goal of life. The real aim of human life is to inquire into the nature of the soul, to realize who we are and what is the summun bonum or the Absolute Truth. This is the proper utility of our intelligence. But how is this done? What is the method by which we can discover this supreme cause? After all, we have imperfect senses and a limited field of experiences.

This is the point at which one should seek the shelter of the bona fide spiritual master. Just as a person seeks out a physician once he discovers he has a disease, so also an intelligent man will be able to perceive that this material world is a miserable place. Everyone here is suffering from birth, death, old age, and disease. No one can escape the stringent laws of material nature. But the difficulty is that even though there is so much suffering due to poor fund of knowledge, the foolish materialists try to make some adjustment within the material energy. So therefore, out of his causeless mercy on such fallen souls, the spiritual master comes to open our eyes, blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge. He is always engaged in service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead. *Mac-cittā mad-gata-prāṇāḥ.* "the thoughts of My pure devotees dwell in Me." (Bg. 10.9) Our beloved spiritual master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, has no other purpose but to deliver the message of *kṛṣṇa-prema* for everyone's benefit. Because of this, he is the most dear to Kṛṣṇa. (Bg. 18.69) Therefore one who aspires for becoming perfect in this life must approach the pure and unalloyed devotee of Kṛṣṇa, His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda, without reservation, and surrender unto him with all faith and humility, and render him transcendental loving service. With this reciprocation established between the pure devotee and the submissive disciple, all auspiciousness is there, and one can be certain that he will soon be going back home, back to Godhead.

*Hare Kṛṣṇa*

## Denver

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Somehow we have gotten the association of a pure devotee of the Lord. He has no faults, he makes no mistakes, his vision is spotlessly pure and he is willing to guide us on the path of eternity. We are blind, of course, but even in this bewildered condition we can see that he is no ordinary saintly man. He is not a *guru* as they are found now, cheaply in the market, but by his symptoms he is a fully realized soul. With perfect intelligence he leads us, patiently training us in the endless subtleties of Kṛṣṇa's service, introducing us with such cautious discretion into the assembly of Kṛṣṇa's intimate association. His mercy cannot be described. The swanlike man has no business in the places where men like us abide. He has no taste for the animal existence which intoxicates our polluted consciousness. And yet he walks among us, speaks to us, accepts our offerings which even men of lesser stature scorn, and remains without quitting the monumental task of reclaiming those who have sunk so low.

In the revealed scriptures there are many recommendations given by the twice-born men of paths leading to the ultimate goal of human life. And there is some conflict as to which of the recommended systems is the best for purifying one's heart. But there is no confusion about the association of these great souls, the pure devotees of the Lord. It is conclusive in all scriptures that one cannot attain success without the association of these men. There is simply no other way.

So, somehow we have gotten the association of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. The revealed scriptures do not boast a more glorious or merciful soul than he. We pray that we may surrender everything we have to him without petty reservation. The debt he has created in us by extending his mercy is fathomless. There should be no talk of business or bargaining. All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your Denver ISKCON servitors*

## Detroit

*Our most dear* Ś*rīla Prabhupāda,*

You are the living force of your devotees. We offer you our eternal obeisances falling at your lotus feet, and there we beg to submit our humble prayers in glorification of your divine qualities, on this most auspicious day of Śrī Vyāsa Pūjā.

We can hardly speak or your glorious attributes; you are the most intimate friend of our Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. How can we ever understand your exalted position? You are favored by Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī and therefore you are the most beloved person in all the three worlds. You have come to deliver us from darkness to light, by freely distributing the nectar of this *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Without considering who is a fit candidate, you have benedicted us with the sweet taste of the holy name. You have given us life; therefore we are eternally bound to you through loving devotional service. Please bless us that we may be able to purely repeat all that you have taught us We are nothing but your peons. We have no qualifications, and are most undeserving, but somehow or other we are being engaged by your mercy only. By receiving your mercy all of our desires are fulfilled. Kṛṣṇa is so kind that He has sent you. O most beloved spiritual master, enable us to understand His kindness. We have no eyes to see, and although we are most dull we are begging for the shade of your lotus feet. For we know that in the most auspicious place, under your perfect guidance, we will actually begin to taste the nectar of the transcendentally sweet eternal life in association with our Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. How can we repay you? We can only offer our lives. Praying at your lotus feet, we offer our obeisances again and again.

*Your eternal servants at Śrī Devasādhana Mandira*

## Fiji

*rahūgaṇaitat tapasā na yāti*  
*na cejyayā nirvapaṇād gṛhād vā*  
*na cchandasā naiva jalāgni-suūryair*  
*vinā mahat-pāda-rajo 'bhiṣekam*

**"My dear King Rahūgaṇa, unless one has the opportunity to smear his entire body with the dust of the lotus feet of the great devotees, one cannot realize the Absolute Truth. One cannot realize the Absolute Truth simply by observing celibacy \[*****brahmacarya*****\], strictly following the rules and regulations of householder life, leaving home as a** ***vānaprastha,*** **accepting** ***sannyāsa,*** **or undergoing severe penances in winter by keeping oneself submerged in water or surrounding oneself in summer by fire and the scorching heat of the sun. There are many other processes to understand the Absolute Truth, but the Absolute Truth is only revealed to one who has attained the mercy of the great devotee." (*****SB*****5.12.12)**

Remembering how you spoke in those beginning days—"Push on this movement, that is my only request. Remain pure and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness." We had no idea then how your energy, purity and compassion would enable peoples in Russia, China and even the Fiji islands to hear the message of Lord Caitanya.

I remember Australia when we first came. Peoples gathered around us: "What are yer, mates? Who's the bloke in the picture?" It was you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we told them of you, of your greatness by serving the Great. We endeavored only to repeat your words and now thousands chant the holy names.

In Fiji we are surrounded again and again with hundreds of eager faces attracted to your books that we hold up for view. Wherever we go people shout "Hare Kṛṣṇa!"

Deoji remembers his first twenty-minute meeting with you in the transit lounge at the airport. You touched his head and asked him to build a temple for Kṛṣṇa. Now that temple is started.

People know you here, Prabhupāda—they know your distinction.

My heart is hurting—but such a good hurt, for I am at your mercy. You have placed me here and now you can make me dance as you like.

Laga sere vua na Kalou! (Sing the names of God!)

*Your servants,*

Upendra dāsa / Deoji Puñja and family / Chitralekhā / Saumya Abhay Charan / Santaya Fjyu

## Gainesville

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our worthless obeisances from beneath the dust of your lotus feet. Although this is the most auspicious of all occasions—the appearance day of the current *ācārya* in the *paramparā* system of disciplic succession—we are still greatly pained by our total lack of ability to express our feelings on this day. This, however, is not due to any academic or intellectual deficiency (indeed, in the material world we were expert at mental speculation and word jugglery), but rather must be attributed to our severe lack of sincerity and general demoniac disposition. Yet, as Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura notes in his poem *Gopinātha,* "many demons have attained your lotus feet." Therefore, in spite of these great defects, Kṛṣṇa has been so kind as to give us a slight glimpse of your transcendental qualities, and we would therefore like to offer a few inadequate words of submission, respects, and appreciation on your appearance day.

In assessing the magnitude of your transcendental qualities, however, we concluded that it would take virtually hundreds of volumes of books to properly describe your transcendental symptoms. What's more is the fact that we are so fallen that we could not even detect such symptoms in the first place. What we would like to do, therefore, is simply list a few of the major qualities which we are able to perceive from within the darkness of our false egos, and hope that you will bestow your causeless mercy upon us in the future, so that we may develop firmer and firmer faith in your lotus feet.

In this connection, we would like to cite a recent incident that best illustrates the quality of your firm dedication to the instructions of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and your own Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. A few weeks ago one of our devotees was doing some shopping in a local store when he met a man who said he knew you when you first came to New York City in 1966. Upon seeing the devotee, the man asked, "Oh, how's Bhaktivedanta Swami these days?" "Oh, he's writing a lot of books," the devotee replied, not knowing what else to say. "Yes," the man said, almost expecting the answer, "he always did a lot of that."

So from this incident, we can understand your transcendental position as the "person *Bhāgavatam*." We did not even know you when this incident originally took place, yet we can see by your actions that you are nondifferent from the instructions which you are giving us right up until the present day. Similarly, sometimes we obtain a copy of some of your older publications, ones published even before you came to America, and in these also, we see that you have been saying the same thing for years and years and years and years, and the message is still exactly verbatim, except perhaps for the technical editing. So this encourages us very greatly, especially since our own propensity is to speculate and twist the real thing, until we have gathered it and molded it to our own perverted desires for sense gratification. So this is perhaps your greatest qualification, as you yourself have said, that you "have not changed even one word of the original message."

Another transcendental quality that is also somewhat illustrated by this incident is your constant emphasis on book distribution. As you may know, at the present time the *karmī* press and media have gone on a "crusade" against Eastern movements in general, and our movement in particular. According to their perception, the leaders of these movements are making a lot of money for their sense gratification, and members in general have been brainwashed and tricked into serving such fallen leaders. As your servants and disciples, we emphatically deny this, and say that it is only their own envy that makes them think like this. Nothing could be further from the truth. The purpose of book distribution is not to make money, but is to spread the knowledge by which the suffering masses can be freed from the miseries of birth, death, disease and old age. Therefore your instructions are to distribute as many books as possible, because simply by reading the knowledge contained in the books, we can actually go home, back to Godhead. Therefore, book distribution is actually the mercy of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu upon the conditioned souls, and you, as His unalloyed devotee and advocate, are hailed at *patita-pāvana,* or the deliverer of many, many fallen souls.

And finally, we must, as your disciples, admit that in spite of so much mercy and good instruction from Your Divine Grace, we are still suffering. Suffering because the knot of false pride and material attachment is so strong that we think that it is actually our real self that we are giving up, instead of our false self, or identification with this material body. In spite of so many offenses, however, you patiently tolerate our nonsense, and continue to drag us back to Kṛṣṇa. Without you we have no position. It the Age of Kali, the only means for spiritual deliverance is the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Nobody is qualified to perform ritualistic sacrifices, or mystic *yoga,* or austerities and penances—the only means is to receive the name of Govinda from the lips of a pure devotee. It is by your mercy only that we even have the little desire that we do. Therefore, you are known as *karuṇā-sindhu,* or an ocean of mercy. We fall at your lotus feet and beg your forgiveness for our past offenses, and pray for a pinch of desire to execute your instructions. Without that, this Vyāsa-Pūjā is simply another exercise in fancy rhetoric, without any practical application. Therefore, please fill our request, and force us to surrender.

*na tathā hy aghavān rājan*  
*pūyeta tapa-ādhibhiḥ*  
*yathā kṛṣṇārpita-prāṇas*  
*tat-puruṣa-niṣevayā*  
*(Bhāg. 6.1.16)*

One cannot be purified merely by undergoing austerity, penance,** ***brahmacarya*** **and the other methods of atonement I have previously described."**

*Your worthless servants,*

## Geneva

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Once in a lecture, you said that when you first met His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, you understood that he was the perfect person to spread Lord Caitanya's movement. As servant of Sarasvatī, we understand that you are the perfect person to take up his mission. Who other than you could present with such force and yet such sweet words the message of the Lord, *gauravāṇī.* There is really no one else, O Jagad-guru!

Weren't we wilder than the beasts of the Jhārikhaṇṇa jungle? And now we are chanting and dancing.

Mahārāja Parīkṣit was going to die in seven days, weren't we already dead? You are our Śukadeva Gosvāmī; through your lips the message of Lord Gaurāṅga becomes sweeter and sweeter.

If Rūpa and Sanātana considered themselves more degraded than Jagāi and Mādhāi, what was our position, what would have been our fate? Nārada Muni saved Mṛgāri; you came to hell to fulfill the orders of your Gurudeva, to pick us up from the ocean of our sinful deeds. With a lot of luck, we would have become trees, to "stand up naked for thousands of years," but by your grace we have been saved like the seven palm trees of South India.

Prabhupāda, we pray to you that we may always remain in the cooling shade of your lotus feet. We pray to keep your instructions as our life and soul, life after life. We are helpless; without your grace we are just falling prey to anger, lust, greed envy and lamentation. Give us the strength. We pray to become and remain pure and to be allowed to assist you in your mission of spreading *kṛṣṇa-prema* all over the world.

Here in Switzerland, people have everything they need materially. As perfect *narādhamas* they are organized like bees and ants. Through your grace, we shall try by giving them Kṛṣṇa, to turn their "zeros" into millions!

*kṛpā-bindu diyā kara 'ei dāse,*
