## Miami

**Gurudeva**

*kṛpa-bindu diyā, koro' ei dāse*  
*tṛṇāpekhā ati hīna*  
*sakala-sahane, bolo diyā koro,*  
*nija-mane spṛhāhīna*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Human life begins when one makes some positive inquiry, searching out the Absolute Truth. The birth of Lord Brahmā was exemplary in this respect because from the very beginning of his life, his only interest was to search out the source of his existence. Our birth, however, was completely inauspicious in all respects, because we were born in the darkness of this age of Kali, at such a time when spiritual activities are in a stage of depression. Beginning from birth our only business has been to seek out all varieties of nescient activities, wasting away the most valuable opportunity which this human life provides. Not caring a fig for spiritual life, somehow or another, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have injected within such fallen and lowly souls the reawakening of a long lost desire to return back to our original constitutional position as eternal servitors of Kṛṣṇa.

Undoubtedly, you are *patita-pāvana,* because no matter how hard we may resist, you are still proceeding to force our eyes open with the torchlight of knowledge. Perhaps one day we will realize that you are actually the external manifestation of Supersoul, bestowing your full blessing to anyone willing to a accept. Hopefully at that time we will take full advantage and bathe in the ocean of your unlimited mercy.

Our fathomless sojourn through this material existence now seems to be coming to an end because by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa you have appeared in our lonely lives. Now by your mercy we are actually entering back into our natural blissful lives of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Not only are you yourself engaged in distributing the unlimited, ever-sweet fruits of Lord Caitanya's tree, but now you are also inviting us to enter into such a sublime *līlā.* In this way, you are displaying all of the real qualifications of a bona fide spiritual master, as you are now practically engaging the whole world in the wonderful pastimes of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Only one decade ago Kṛṣṇa was practically unknown to everyone. Now the entire globe is beginning to vibrate with the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Even the most callous souls are becoming unable to resist, and the greatest skeptics are beginning to accept. Truly, if anyone wants to see miracles, he need only open his eyes.

Although we are unable to fully appreciate your actual position, we are somehow able to perceive that you are our real father to whom we are eternally indebted. We are just like young children who don't even know what is good or bad, or what is right or wrong, and you are so tolerant, taking us by the hand and completely guiding us step by step on this most important voyage home. All of our former parents and teachers had no conception at all of how one should make proper use of this human form of life. They were only able to direct us in the matter of increasing our material bondage through greater and greater sense gratification. This is the greatest pitfall which causes one to completely lose all sanity and ultimately fall victim to the forest of material enjoyment. Actually, we had become faithful servants to masters we were never able to satisfy.

Now our lives are becoming fresh and new as we continue day by day to follow your simple instructions. By refraining from undesirable habits and chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* you are enabling us to realize the topmost confidential knowledge with is not even to be found in all the Vedic scriptures. It is wait that by becoming the servant of a king one is able to gain the benefit of enjoying the whole kingdom. Similarly, by us becoming your servants, you are offering us eternal entry into the limitless ocean of love of Godhead.

Knowing now that our lives are meant only for serving you, we pray that you may recognize our puny efforts and somehow or another be pleased by them. Our only request to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that you may always allow us to remain in the shelter of your lotus feet.

## Montreal

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We were sleeping in the night of ignorance, and you came to awaken us to the reality of our true existence. Busy at so many shadow roles, we never understood who we were, blinded by the cloud of material enjoyment, imprisoned in *māyā's* alien atmosphere. But your potent words pierced through the darkness of illusion, and we have now been delivered. Thus in a light of your purity, we could catch a glimpse of the realm which we now know to be Vṛndāvana, the abode of unalloyed love, the land of *cintāmaṇi* where absolute knowledge and bliss manifest eternally.

Like the father who brings back home his disobedient children gone astray, you are bringing us back to our spiritual health and sanity, and inspiring us with the desire to enter into the eternal loving service of Kṛṣṇa. You are showering us with the most priceless gift of pure devotional service. With no means of measuring or fulfilling our debt to you, we still beg to be accepted as your menial maidservants.

As your own desire is to serve the desire of your eternal father, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, to preach the message of Śrī Caitanya in the West, we also pray to make your desire ours; thus our life will know perfection, by serving the perfect preceptor. You are the emblem of all knowledge; you are the effulgent *mahātmā* who can awaken within the hearts of even the most degraded *yavanas* and *mlecchas* attraction for devotional service. You are the embodiment of all perfection. We derive great pleasure in remembering your ambrosial body, always decorated with gracious motions. Looking at your form and hearing your words are the perfection of life because they are direct manifestations of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. The scriptures describe that the pure devotee is always meditating on the Lord and the Lord is also constantly thinking of His unalloyed devotee. We also pray at your lotus feet to be graced with constant remembrance of your instructions, nondifferent from you, and thus engage ourselves wholeheartedly in spreading the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya wherever has not been heard your magnanimous name.

*Jaya Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda Mahārāja.*

*Your servants,*

Nandikeśvara dāsa, Mandarī-devī dāsī, Subhadrā, Rādhārāṇī / Bhartṛhari dāsa, Sarada-devī dāsī / Hanumān dāsa, Bhakta-devī dāsī, Gaṅgāmāyi, Bhaktivedanta, Yamunāmāyi / Aāyāpurśāsa dāsa, Sarvamāyi-devī dāsī, Sanātana / Jitavrata dāsa, Rādhānarupiṇī-devī dāsī / Keśiśatru dāsa, Medhyā-devī dāsī / Dharmarāja dāsa, Karen / Praghoṣa dāsa brahmacārī / Gokulānanda dāsa brahmacārī / Jitarati dāsa brahmacārī / Vyāpaka dāsa brahmacārī / Padmapāṇi dāsa brahmacārī / Ghoṣa Ṭhākura dāsa brahmacārī / Priyabandhu dāsa brahmacārī / Jaya Govinda dāsa brahmacārī / Vidyārāja dāsa brahmacārī / Sadāpuṣṭa dāsa brahmacārī / Parangara dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Yvan / Bhaktin Alain / Bhakta Richard / Jyotirāditya-devī dāsī / Jaganmātā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Line

## Nairobi

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear very merciful Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We long to glorify you as you deserve, at all times and especially on this most blessed occasion of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā. Please be patient and accept the dumb feeling of gratitude with which we offer homage, and not the halting illiterate expressions. When Dhruva Mahārāja desired to glorify the Supreme Lord but lacked the words to do so, the Lord kindly touched his forehead with His conch, and Dhruva was able to offer nice prayers. Alas, in our abominable, godless culture we have no one to instruct us how to approach you, except you yourself. Please look kindly upon our endeavor. What little we can say and do is by your grace only.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

We were born in the darkest ignorance, and you, our beloved Gurudeva, are opening our eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. We offer our respectful obeisances unto you. We are in such ignorance that we cannot tell light from darkness. Without your constant guidance, we are unable to perceive the danger of our position, hopelessly, helplessly attached as we are to our vain efforts to lord it over the inexorable, insurmountable material energy. We read that:

*guru-kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.45).**

Even knowing this, we are quite unqualified to approach you, to glorify you, or to offer any service to you.

You have written that "the expert spiritual master knows well how to engage his disciples' energy in the transcendental loving service of the Lord, and thus he engages a devotee on a specific devotional service according to his special tendency." Your supremely distinguished spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja, regarded anyone as fallen who was not chanting sixty-four rounds a day. It is your supreme mercy on fallen wretches like ourselves that, pitying our lack of attachment to chanting, you accept us al your disciples if we chant only sixteen rounds a day. You have so kindly devised a limitless range of transcendental activity to engage our propensities and our slight attachment to devotional service.

*sādhana-bhakti haite haya premera udgama*

**"By practicing this regulated devotional service under the direction of the spiritual master, certainly one awakens his dormant love of Godhead."**

The miracle is that, deeply embedded though we are in sinful activities, speech and thought, we are actually becoming purified by the process of devotional service which you have given us. (*Cc*. *Ādi* 7.142)

How can we enumerate the joys of this real spiritual life, which is your gift to us? Deity worship, offering *ārātrika,* preaching, distribution, cooking for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, dancing and chanting jubilantly with *mṛdaṅga* and *karatālas*—these and so many other activities are deeply enlivening us, and drawing is from the pursuit of dangerous, so-called pleasure within the jurisdiction of *māyā.* By following this natural, enjoyable process we are actually becoming transcendentalists, actually realizing that we are not these material bodies but that we are spirit souls with an eternal relationship with Kṛṣṇa. You are really giving us Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, as master, friend, even as our child or as our beloved.

Kṛṣṇa sits under the trees in Vaṁśivaṭa, attracting the hearts of the *gopīs* with His flute. The vibration of that transcendental flute, entering the ears of Lord Brahmā, the creator of the material universe, was expressed by him as the *kāma-gāyatrī.* This Gāyatrī *mantra* has been passed from master to disciple in the *paramparā,* and now you, the outward manifestation of Kṛṣṇa within our hearts, are initiating us with it, and attracting us back to Kṛṣṇa, the transcendental Cupid, inviting us to take part in His eternal blissful pastimes in the spiritual sky.

In the midst of the holocaust of this materialistic life, we are beginning to have some realization of eternal life full of variegated blissful activity. This is your miracle, that you are pulling *yavanas* and *mlecchas* off the streets and showing them the way to attain the topmost transcendental platform of pure love of God.

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**"The real import of the scriptures is revealed to one who has unflinching faith in both the Supreme Personality of Godhead and the spiritual master."**

In *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* it is stated that real faith is the complete conviction that simply by serving the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa one can attain all perfection. Serving you is as good as serving Kṛṣṇa: *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādhā.* By satisfying you, Kṛṣṇa is also satisfied. Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja has stated that, "One can become perfectly successful in the mission of his life if he acts exactly according to the words he hears from his spiritual master." Because we are lacking in this faith, we are constantly in illusion that we can improve your sublime instructions and thus the potency of your mission to benedict the entire world with Kṛṣṇa consciousness is impaired.

*edākā āmāra, nāhi pāya bolo*  
*harināma-saṅkīrtane*  
*tumi kṛpa kori', śraddhā-bindu diyā*  
*deha' kṛṣṇa-nāma-dhane*

We do not find the strength to carry on alone with this *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana.* Please bless us by giving us just one drop of faith with which to obtain the great treasure of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

*namaste sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*

Our respectful obeisances are unto you, the devoted servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, who are preaching the message of Lord Caitanya. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is Kṛṣṇa Himself, descending in the mood of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī to teach us perfect devotion and love for Kṛṣṇa. He was so much attached to his spiritual master that he collected earth from his birthsite and ate a little of it every day. He was a Vedantist (*vedānta-kṛd veda-vid eva cāham:* By the *Vedas* I am to be known; indeed, I am the compiler of *Vedānta,* and I am the knower of the *Vedas.*") and yet when His spiritual master called Him a fool, unqualified to study Vedānta philosophy, and instructed Him to chant the holy name of Kṛṣṇa at all times, he humbly followed these simple instructions.

*ei tāṇra vākye āmi dṛḍha viśvāsa dhari'*  
*nirantara kṛṣṇa-nāma saṅkīrtana kari*

**"I firmly believe in these words of my spiritual master, and therefore I always chant the holy name of the Lord, alone and in the association of devotees." (Cc.** ***Ādi*** **7.95)**

Here in Kenya, where programs such as large-scale book distribution are hardly practicable, the instructions which you have kindly given us are simply those with which Śrī Īśvara Purī benedicted his beloved disciple Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya dāsa brahmacārī

*nāca, gāo, bhakta-saṅge kara saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma upadeśi' tāra' sarva jana*

**"My dear child, continue dancing, chanting and performing** ***saṅkīrtana*** **in the association of devotees. Furthermore, go out and preach the value of chanting** ***kṛṣṇa-nāma,*** **for by this process you will be able to deliver all fallen souls." As a result of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu's faithful adherence to the instructions of His spiritual master "the food of love of Godhead swelled in all directions, and thus young men, old men, women and children were all immersed in that inundation." (Cc.** ***Ādi*** **7.92)**

*śrī guru kāruṇa-sindhu*

We are also drowning in the sweet ocean of your mercy. During your last glorious memorable visit to Nairobi you instructed us how to preach carefully and humbly. You told us who we should approach people of all sorts "with a straw in our mouths," and how to flatter them wisely. You said we should invite them humbly to throw away all the nonsense they have learned and to follow the instructions of Lord Caitanya "Just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa." We laughed with delight and you were also laughing, as you described how even "first-class loafers and rascals could become interested in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and take up chanting if we preach like this." You instructed us that we should have "four-hour *kīrtanas* and distribute sumptuous *prasāda.*"

We are very much desirous that this flood of *kṛṣṇa-prema,* ecstatic love or Kṛṣṇa, sweep over the whole continent of Africa. We want to see thousands of fallen conditioned souls experiencing the transcendental pleasure of *śrī harināma-saṅkīrtana-prasādānanda,* the real bliss of chanting and dancing for Kṛṣṇa, and taking the sacred remnants of His foodstuffs.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we humbly pray that you will forgive all our offenses, we have no seriousness to satisfy you and to fulfill your desires. Still we beg that you will continue to shower us with your mercy so that we can have the faith, enthusiasm and determination to carry on with this mission. Please bless us so that we can have *dṛḍha-vrata*—determined faith and *śrota-vākya,* acceptance of your words, so that we can carry out your instructions without deviation. In this way the chanting and dancing will go on nicely, the people of Africa will be benefited and we will become purified, and by your grace make advancement in devotional service.

Praying that Lord Kṛṣṇa will bless Your Divine Grace with long life and good spirits to carry on with your wonderful translations and purports to the transcendental Vaiṣṇava literatures, and with this great work of benedicting the entire world with Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we humbly offer our prostrate *daṇḍavats* at your most holy lotus feet.

Brahmānanda Svāmī, GBC

Navyogendra Svāmī (Mombasa) / Jñāna dāsa, Līlāvatī-devī dāsī / Taponidhi dāsa adhikārī / Yogīśa dāsa adhikārī / Rāsalīlā-devī dāsī / Ajāmila dāsa brahmacārī / Hasyānanda dāsa brahmacārī (Mombasa) / Jalakara dāsa brahmacārī / Mahāvirya dāsa brahmacārī / Naradeva dāsa brahmacārī / Sanātana dāsa brahmacārī / Sarvavid dāsa brahmacārī (Mombasa) / Tribhaṅgānanda dāsa brahmacārī / Pārvatī-devī dāsī / Rādhikā-devī dāsī / Śucī-devī dāsī / Yaśasvinī-devī dāsī

## New Orleans

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We fall again and again at your lotus feet. By your causeless mercy we are finding the strength to serve your lotus feet.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge."**

O beloved Gurudeva, amidst the ocean of unlimited frustration and anxiety where billions of souls are drowning, you are sailing. You have so much compassion for the plagued population that your mission is simply to save these insane sinners. Any one of them can take shelter of your mercy but due to their being tossed by the waves of this ocean (*sattva, rajas* and *tamo-guṇa*) it is very difficult. But a few phenomenally fortunate sufferers catch hold of the life-saving seeds which you cast twenty-four hours daily for the rescue of the blind-visioned billions.

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

O Jagad-guru, our debt to you will never be paid even in a million millenniums. All we can do is beg and beg that you please let us stay with you forever.

Even if every tongue on the infinite-headed Anantadeva were chanting "thank you Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you," it would not be sufficient to express our gratitude, O Paramahaṁsa Ṭhākura.

*namo namas te 'stu sahasra-kṛtvaḥ*  
*punaś ca bhūyo 'pi namo namas te*  
*namaḥ purastād atha pṛṣṭhatas te,*  
*namo 'stu te sarvata eva sarvaḥ*

**Thus I offer my respectful obeisances unto you a thousand times, and again and yet again! Obeisances from the front, from behind and from all sides! (*****Bg*****. 11.39-40)**

New Orleans

## New Vṛndāvana

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura writes, *yogyatā-vicāre kichu nāhi paī, tomāra karuṇā-sāra:* "If you examine me, you will find no good qualities. Your mercy is all that I am made of." Individually or collectively, there is nothing to be said in our favor except that we are your disciples. By your mercy we can accomplish anything, but without Your Grace all of our so-called achievements are useless.

Our only business in life is to somehow or other regain your favor. First of all we have to receive you suitably, then we will be able to hear your message attentively. We have been instructed to accept you in our hearts to be as good as the Supreme Lord Himself: *sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair uktas tahtā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ.* Now we wish to receive you in our community in like manner.

As a body is rendered lifeless without the presence of the spirit soul, there is no meaning to New Vrindaban without your presence. Of course, we can understand that even without your actual physical presence you are still residing here. But it is all based upon desire. You will not choose to remain where you are not properly respected. Therefore we are trying to build you a palace.

We are always in a dependent position. We may have some desire but we are powerless to fulfill it by ourselves. All we can do is pray unto the lotus feet of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Vrindaban Candra to allow us to finish your palace this year. Then you may bless us by coming here and making everything perfect—our working, our lives, and our entire community.

Kīrtanānanda Svāmī and all the inmates of New Vrindaban

## New York/Rādhā-Dāmodara TSKP

*Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

One day in Vṛndāvana I ventured, "None of our men want to leave here."

You replied, "Yes, actually no one wants to leave here."

Yet, somehow or other, the desires had arisen and we had fallen away.

*icchā-dveṣa samutthena*  
*dvandva-mohena bhārata*  
*sarva-bhūtāni sammohaṁ*  
*sarge yānti parantapa*  
*(Bg. 7.27)*

Although devotional activity is completely pure, still we are just now rising from the mine of fear and hate. Thus in contrast to your spotless purity, we stand embarrassed by filthy garments of material desire that cover our long camouflaged forms, hindering our motions as we struggle to take advantage of your mercy.

Sometimes, when a disease of the blood causes boils to erupt on the otherwise placid skin of society, you do not cry for the removal of the sores, but instead, treat the affliction within. And when the contagion of fear and hate begins to spread its full epidemic proportion, you are the all-knowing, undisturbed, universal physician, patiently dispensing Śrī Caitanya's prescription of *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana.*

When we had lost our way in the dense forest of delusion due to an unreasonable desire to taste all the fruit there, you appeared, just to lead u home.

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*  
*\[Cc. Madhya 19.151\]*

Although we were once all good and loving sons of the Supreme Father, we became spiteful wanderers amongst the groves of bitter fruits, hopelessly seeking out that sweet fruit that grows in the lush courtyards of our fathers' kingdom. Never lost or disconsolate, you are dragging us back to His abode, although the fruits that you offer taste bitter to our still jaundiced tongues, and thus we complain endlessly as we follow.

Now, as we fall at your feet like responsible children, exhausted from lifetimes of capricious sporting, senseless of everything beyond your shelter, you shower forth your mercy in incessant torrents, consoling and promising that we will never wander from Vṛndāvana again, as you have awakened the seeds of that Vṛndāvana within our hearts.

All glories to Your Divine Grace. May we somehow learn to accept your mercy.

*Your servants at New York ISKCON—Rādhā-Dāmodara TSKP*

## Ottawa

The land of Bhārata-varṣa was once a paradise, with truthfulness  
cleanliness, compassion and mercy standing supreme.  
Indeed even the threefold miseries of life were not to be seen  
Yes, this land of Bhārata-varṣa was once very nice.  
Advancing through Satya, Tretā and Dvāpara yugas,  
man's good qualities were gradually diminished.  
Kali was now suddenly upon us, flourishing with asuras.  
Only truthfulness remained to be vanquished.  
Oh, Gurudeva, even though the age of Kali was just beginning,  
the whole planet was so quickly degrading. The only trace  
of civilization to be found in India, Kṛṣṇa holy ground.  
It was Lord Caitanya's desire to restore this sanātana-dharma  
And this He came to inspire through the Hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana.  
But the age of Kali was so insistent, that few men realized  
its significance. What was inspired in the East, would  
Ultimately flourish in the West.  
And so it was that at an advanced age of life, you left the  
holy dhāma of Vṛndāvana to boldly confront Kali personified  
like entering a cage of angry lions.  
Your only weapon—faith in the words of your Guru Mahārāja.  
Your determination—to make Vaiṣṇavas our of yavanas.  
Your first year in America—filled with shortcomings and  
frustrations. Few friends, poor shelter, cold weather.  
Yet you stayed holding fast to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.  
The tides must be turned, the battle must be won.  
For ten years now you have fought relentlessly.  
You are forever unflinching in your determination.  
In this way you remain a source of inspiration.  
Even this most reluctant soul is becoming a devotee.  
But the battle has yet to be won.  
Hence you are always pushing us on.  
With the sword of Vedic knowledge shining bright.  
You are cutting māyā's ropes which are binding us tight.  
Oh, Prabhupāda you are most intimate when writing your books.  
Indeed your very presence is felt on every page.  
By diving deeply into that ocean of nectar  
The whole world can gain the association of the greatest sage.  
Our dearmost eternal father,  
We are drowning in an ocean of misery.  
You are the friend of the poor  
Kindly bestow upon us a drop of your mercy.  
Oh, Gurudeva, with you and Kṛṣṇa on our side  
We will restore this land of Bhārata-varṣa.  
We will reestablish the principles of religion  
We will overflood the universe with prema-bhakti.  
Our beloved Prabhupāda, please forgive us  
For all the offenses we are committing at your lotus feet.  
We offer our prostrated obeisances to you again and again.  
We beg you to let us engage in your service for eternity.

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You are present in your books, which are now being read everywhere around the world. Therefore you are as good as the all-pervasive Supersoul. In fact you are more merciful, because Supersoul directs everyone to satisfy their desires. Sometimes these desires drag them into hellish conditions of live, or sometimes to elevated levels of material enjoyment. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are a pure, unalloyed devotee of Kṛṣṇa. You can plant in the heart of the conditioned soul the seed of *bhakti.* This is your real mercy. A conditioned soul may travel for millions and millions of births in all species and realms of the material world, but he can never come to the point of devotional service. It is only by association with a pure devotee such as Your Divine Grace that one can enter the field of devotional service. By reading your books and following your instructions, we can have your association twenty-four hours a day.

It is now up to us to realize your unlimited greatness. You are a dear friend and intimate associate of Kṛṣṇa, as Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura prays:

*nikuñja-yūnr rati-keli-siddhyai*  
*yā yālibhir yuktir apekṣaṇīyā*  
*tatrāti-dākṣyād ati vallabhasya*  
*vande guroḥ cṛī caraṇāravindam*

Your greatness is that you are the *ācārya* and by your perfect example you can show us how to become intimate as associates of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

We can understand from your presentation of *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* that personal association with Kṛṣṇa and sometimes separation from Kṛṣṇa are the most transcendentally relishable mellows. Now you are allowing us to taste this fruit of love of God. By experiencing this mellow we, who have a long developed attraction for sense gratification, have give up the four pillars of sinful life, namely, intoxication, meat-eating, mental speculation, and illicit sex. It is your mercy, the taste of *prema,* that is allowing us to do so.

We pray to Kṛṣṇa for the intelligence to understand your books. This understanding is the key to the shackle of the material energy. You are opening the storehouse of love of God and are freely distributing it to everyone.

This collection of praises from devotees around the world shows how you have made many different people unite and become resolute in purpose. Perhaps our humble attempts at broadcasting your fathomless glories will encourage you to be merciful upon us. Without your mercy we have noting, with it we have everything, Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants,*

## Paris and New Māyāpur

To His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, the *ācārya* of the world, who is transforming this earth into the abode of Lord Kṛṣṇa, who is ever merciful to the fallen conditioned souls.

*guru-kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.45).**

Your Divine Grace, you are the empowered representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa. As such you carry the conclusion of all Vedic knowledge ("Kṛṣṇa, God, is great and we are small; the small should serve the great."), thus you are able to make *kṛṣṇa-bhaktas* all over the world. Being fully saturated with service and devotion to Kṛṣṇa, wherever you stay, temples and devotees of Kṛṣṇa spring up. Tirelessly you are kindling the dampened souls of the age of Kali with your contagious enthusiasm for the message of Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Your presence is like a strong wind that is clearing away the foul atmosphere of sense gratification and sin. You have wounded the personality of Kali by deeply implanting the eternal principles of *bhāgavata-dharma.* Your presence is the essence of hope and devotees all over the world are cheering your transcendental activities. All glories to the wandering swanlike devotee, the great preceptor Śrī Śrīmān A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who has take complete shelter at the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our supreme leader, *bando mui sāvadhāna mate.* We look to you with great awe and reverence and want to model ourselves after you. You have freed us from the insanity if demoniac life and have given us a new birth into the spiritual world of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thinking ourselves to be God, we were but proud beggars. Being born of sixth class families, having no pious activities, austerity or penance in mind, being bankrupt of spiritual knowledge, we had no attraction for serving the supreme master and hero of Vṛndāvana, Lord Govinda. Now after hearing the transcendental sounds emanating from your lotus mouth, we are enthused for going back home, back to Godhead and for leaving aside our sinful activities. Please give us the strength to continue to follow your giant footsteps. O merciful master, you have dragged us from the womb of *māyā* and have given us our real human birth. Please keep us steady in devotional service that we may not again become the toy of birth and death. We pray for devotion to the lotus feet of Guru and Gaurāṅga, and that we may always see you clearly in everything we do.

## Philadelphia

We humbly try on Vyāsa-Pūjā day  
To honor Your Divine Grace  
Deliverer of the Western world  
So fallen in disgrace  
We're in the age of Kali-yuga  
A time of pain and sorrow  
Irreligious life prevails  
In an atmosphere of quarrel  
Amidst the chaos and confusion  
Your Divine Grace arrived  
To preach the message of Mahāprabhu  
Compassion personified  
Lord Caitanya's advent is rare  
He comes once in Brahmā's day  
In His disciplic line you stand  
To teach what scriptures say  
No weapons did you bring along  
Only the holy name  
On the order of your Gurudeva  
You've expanded Kṛṣṇa fame  
Jaya, Jagad-guru!  
Savior of the Kali-yuga  
Jaya, Jagad-guru!  
All glories to you!  
Philosopher of Vedic wisdom  
Scholar of the age  
Your books bring light into the world  
A new dawn page to page  
Your translation of Bhagavad-gītā  
Makes the meaning at last clear  
And your purports to Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam  
Have freed our hearts from fear  
The theory that life comes from matter  
Is accepted without proof  
But you have shown the soul to be  
Superior and aloof  
You've taught that there's a difference  
Between God and the soul  
The relationship of the two exists  
As a part is to the whole  
Jaya, Jagad-guru!  
Savior of the Kali-yuga  
Jaya, Jagad-guru!  
All glories to you!  
Your morning walk conversations  
Enliven everyone  
(As you walk, your head appears to be  
Held higher than the sun)  
The mystic sages can't approach  
The position you've attained  
The qualities of a pure devotee  
Are the highest honors gained  
O ācārya of the universe  
How deep your plans do dwell  
To save all fallen, conditioned souls  
In heaven and in hell  
This godless age will not prevail  
Your missions a success  
The holy name which you have sung  
Has touched our consciousness  
Jaya, Jagad-guru!  
Savior of the Kali-yuga  
Jaya, Jagad-guru!  
All glories to you!

## Portland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this most holy day of your divine appearance, we, of New Maṇipura, do beg to fall down in the dust of your lotus feet, along with all our Godbrothers from around the world, and offer our crippled and depraved selves in service to the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya. In the revealed scriptures it is declared that the spiritual master is to be worshiped as God because he is the most confidential servant of God. Therefore, we should write sixty volumes of glorification of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we could easily do it, since your glories are limitless, but we are simply praying that you will accept these few lines which are like offering a match to the sun.

In the *Bhagavad-gītā* it is said, "Out of many thousands of men one will endeavor for perfection, and out of those who have achieved perfection, only one may know Me in truth." Only in our wildest dreams could we have ever thought that we would get one of those very rare souls as our spiritual master. We had no qualification to approach such a rare soul, being addicted to meat-eating, illicit sex, gambling and drugs. We thought ourselves happy in such a degraded condition and we were marching off to hell.

While others were content to sit and chant, only you were moved by the compassion characteristic of the real Vaiṣṇava, the person who really knows Kṛṣṇa in truth. Simply by making a humble effort to serve the lotus feet of your divine master you have relieved us from chewing the chewed again and again. It was actually the desire of Lord Caitanya that anyone who takes his birth in India should spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement throughout the world, but again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, only you, as a true vaiṣṇava, servant of the Lord, have submitted humbly to the Lord's orders. In the *Bhagavad-gītā* Lord Kṛṣṇa says that there is no servant dearer to him than the one who teaches the message of the *Bhagavad-gītā.* Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, had the determination to become most dear to Kṛṣṇa; only you had the courage to leave the holy *dhāma* and set foot to the grizzly Kali-yuga hill of America, just to rescue a few rotten conditioned souls—our lowly selves.

Even if we had millions of births it would not be possible to repay you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your compassion, determination, courage and mercy. You released us from the muck of sense gratification into the cool, refreshing waters of satisfying the Lord. We are simply praying that somehow or other you engage us in assisting you in your holy mission of saving the world from hell, and we pray that whatever awkward and meager service we do will be accepted by you. Only in this way can we advance, for all the great authorities have confirmed that only by satisfying the spiritual master can one get the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.

Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, rolling in the dust of your feet we beg for your mercy.

*Your lowly servants in New Maṇipura*

## Puerto Rico

Just as Priyavrata rode a chariot  
And was radiant like the sun.  
So your resplendent mercy has now come,  
To continue what Caitanya Mahāprabhu had begun.  
The saṅkīrtana movement is going  
To every town and village,  
The devotees are traveling on an international pilgrimage,  
To awaken every sleeping soul to his heritage.  
Sons of Kṛṣṇa, awaken to the sound  
Of Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu, whose mercy knows no limit,  
Now with khole blazing and his karatālas shimmering.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda has come to us to give it.  
What can I do to serve you,  
I bang my head against the wall,  
I have never done anything at all,  
I'm hardly a servant, I'm so small, small small!  
Like an insect at the bottom of a well,  
Whose voice is nothing amidst a chorus of others,  
I beg to serve you with all my Godbrothers,  
We will help you by pulling others from māyā's gutters.  
Please Prabhupāda, I want to open my eyes,  
And in the pure light of love see you,  
If to your instructions I am always true  
I know Kṛṣṇa will give me intelligence of what I have to do.  
My only desire is to be your servant,  
I am helpless without my spiritual master,  
I will never leave; like a plaster  
I will remain glued to your instructions for ever after.  
On this Vyāsa-pūjā, there is nothing I have to give,  
You already own my life and soul,  
Body, mind, and words, my goal  
Is to surrender all, and become full.  
Please Prabhupāda, please do not kick me away,  
Without you there is no night, there is no day.

ISKCON Puerto Rico

Jagajīvana dāsa / All the assembled devotees

## Rome

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Śrīla Prabhupāda!*

It is very difficult of us to express our gratefulness to you by words, so we are trying to do it by practical activity. Actually, that is the only way that we can show how to sincerely be thankful for what you are giving is.

In one of your letters to us, you said,

"There is very good scope in Europe for this *saṅkīrtana* movement. If you just spread this chanting in Europe, then it becomes Vaikuṇṭha. Side by side push on the literature distribution as far as possible. This should be done very vigorously and you will have great success there. My Guru Mahārāja was always very pleased when even a small pamphlet was distributed."

As we want to please you, please you because we want more and more of your association. You are the only relationship we have, the only person we want to listen to. And pleasing you means to dive deep into the ocean of your smile, your instructions.

We want to whole of Italy to scream as loud as possible from the bottom of their lungs "Tutte le glorie a Śrīla Prabhupāda!" (All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!).

And in the ecstasy of this desire we want to beg you to be so merciful towards us so that this feeling will grow become reality.

*Hoping to become your sincere servants,*

The devotees of Rome temple

## San Diego

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We fall at your lotus feet, for in this dangerous jungle of material life your association provides the only sanctuary for our depraved and crippled selves. Before coming to Kṛṣṇa consciousness, although having for ages been dulled by the spell of *māyā* and devoid of all sense of well-being, we were nevertheless looked up to by our fellow *mlecchas* and *yavanas* as having some good qualification. In actuality, however, we were completely ignorant of any distinction between good and bad, happiness and distress, right and wrong, pleasure and pain, and were all marching obliviously towards the darkest, most hellish regions of the universe. Then you checked our ill-fated course and saved us from millions and millions of births of suffering in hell:

*śrī-guru karuṇā sindhu,*  
*adhama jarāra bandhu,*  
*lokanātha lokera jīvana*

**"Our spiritual master is the ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor, and the lord and master of the devotees."**

*uadekṣyanti te jñānaṁ*  
*jñāninas tattva-darśinaḥ*

**"The self-realized soul can impart knowledge unto you because he has seen the truth. (Bg. 4.34)**

You are the real *guru.* Your words are not simply cheap boasts or the concocted fantasy of some pretender. You are always displaying your qualification by showing us the reality if a personal God and the blissful transcendental realm as it is described in the scriptures, and by giving us this *saṅkīrtana* movement which spreads factual realization of the spiritual realm. You are fulfilling the prediction in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* that the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will transform every town and village all over the world. What demons dare to deny your superexellence?

Association with a pure devotee is rarely achieved. We are such fools, however, that we have barely accepted a minute fraction of your mercy. And because this reluctance prevents us from properly qualifying as your devotees, we know that it is only by your unlimited magnanimity that we have been able to continue in your service. Realizing our deficiency, we pray to you for the power to relish and broadcast the glories of the Lord, and to effectively heed your repeated warnings of present and forthcoming danger within this material world. We also beg to be able to execute this service with a pure heart, free from all offenses, which only serve to hinder your divine mission and waste your precious time.

Independently, we can do nothing, for we can barely see beyond our own senses, what to speak of understanding how to best engage the entire universal population at Kṛṣṇa's lotus feet. Thus, in full cooperative association, we must take complete shelter of your perfect direction and vision. Only through this unified effort, free from all selfish motivation, can we hope to be instrumental in the successful execution of your plan. In this way, the whole world will have facility to develop a feeling for that loving transcendental mood that we so conspicuously lack and that you so perfectly display. Praying at your lotus feet for forgiveness for our innumerable offenses, we remain...

*Your eternally indebted servitors.*

The devotees of ISKCON San Diego

## Santo Domingo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*Our dear Gurudeva,*

Please accept our most fallen and unworthy obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

Years ago we were completely sleeping in the lap of *māyā,* living like cats and dogs—eating, sleeping, mating and defending. Suddenly Kṛṣṇa sent His mercy to be spread through His representatives: the six Gosvāmīs. That was the same mercy of Lord Caitanya, who is none other than Śrī Kṛṣṇa Himself. And you, divine master, are his genuine representative. As the fools deride Kṛṣṇa when He descends in His human form of life, similarly we the fools number one didn't know about your glories and potency. We knew only that we were given knowledge (*vidyā*), pure foodstuff (*prasāda*), and the most valuable *mahā-mantra* to be chanted, the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra.* At first we were completely unable to understand anything except that everything you were so kindly giving us without motivation was very pleasing to all. We put just a little faith in what you were teaching. Not knowing the seriousness of your personality, we began trying to follow the instructions that you had given to your advanced disciples. And that was so powerful that even without your physical presence everything became as bright as the sun. Our hearts are enlightened by your mercy, which spreads through the three worlds as the Ganges water flows from Nārāyaṇa's feet. We begin to live again. Now, as time has passed, by practical experience we see ourselves different, and that difference is your mercy.

O Gurudeva, you have changed our hearts by cleaning it with your purity. Without asking anything in return, you have given us an eternal and blissful life, full of knowledge and satisfaction. If there seems to be something more valuable than this, it is not absolute, surely. Please, spiritual master, excuse us because we are still not able to repay you for what you have given us, and we are helpless in this regard. Please, we beg you, accept this useless offering on your day of days.

*Your servants,*

ISKCON Santa Domingo

## Santiago

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It is only through devotional service that a conditioned soul, who is tormented by thousands of sinful desires, can purify himself and thus understand Śrī Kṛṣṇa the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Your mercy is spread all over the three worlds. That mercy has touched us and today we are able to chant the holy names of the Lord and engage ourselves in His service. Otherwise, how could such an impudent mundane enjoyer and fallen soul such as myself pronounce the sacred names of the Lord. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are like a merciful sword that cuts the hard know of the heart (*hṛdaya-granthi*). You are the manifestation of the causeless mercy of the Supreme Lord, who so kindly is dragging the conditioned souls out of the loneliness and darkness of this material existence.

Oh, eternal Father! Be merciful with us and permit us to transfer our attachments to your lotus feet, which are our only oasis in the desert of the material creation.

*prasaṅgam ajaraṁ pāśam*  
*ātmānaḥ kavayo viduḥ*  
*sa eva sādhuṣu kṛto*  
*mokṣa-dvāram apāvṛtam*

**"All men of knowledge know very well that material attachment is the greatest entanglement of the soul, but this same attachment, when it is transferred to the self-realized devotees, opens the door of liberation." (*****Bhāg.*** **3.25.20)**

ISKCON Chile

Viraha Prakaśa Svāmī

## Seattle

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās*  
*tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthīkurvanti tīrthānī*  
*svāntaḥ-stena gadābhṛtā*

**Śrīla Prabhupāda, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage. (B*****hāg.*** **1.13.10)**

In meditation on this verse as we left Māyāpur and Vṛndāvana to return to our service in other parts of the world we are appreciating the value of this pilgrimage. To go to some holy place of pilgrimage does not mean only to take a bath in the Ganges or Yamunā or to visit the temples situated in those places. One must seek out and take advantage of the pure devotees who reside at such places. The holy places are meant of purifying the polluted consciousness of the human being by an atmosphere surcharged with the presence of the Lord's unalloyed devotees. The personality of Godhead is always with such pure devotees because of their unalloyed service, which is without any tinge of fruitive action. The Personality of Godhead is omnipresent by His diverse potencies everywhere within space, but the Lord's omnipresence is perceived and manifested by His unalloyed devotees like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, just as electricity is manifested in an electric bulb. Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel that it has been by your mercy and for your mercy only that there has been any value to this annual pilgrimage.

Not only are the holy places worthy of the name only on account of the pure devotees residing there, but also actions of the pure devotees can render any place into a place of pilgrimage. Such pure devotees are able to rectify the polluted atmosphere of any place rendered unholy by the questionable actions of interested persons who try to adopt a professional life at the cost of the reputation of the holy place.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were comfortably situated in your rooms at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara temple, but you chose to leave to fulfill the order of your spiritual master and preach the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Yet in your leaving, you took Vṛndāvana with you—within your heart and within the pure chanting of the holy names of the Lord. Therefore, by your leaving and crisscrossing this entire planet, millions, nay, unlimited numbers of fallen conditioned souls are getting the opportunity to experience Vṛndāvana. This is your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

There are two ways of association with the spiritual master—*vapu* and *vāṇī. Vapu* means to be in his personal presence and *vāṇī* means to associate with him through his teachings by following his instructions. Of those two *vāṇī* is better. If a man is lost and confused in a dense forest and in such a hopeless condition he meets a woodsman who is familiar with the forest and knows its paths, then such a lost man simply has to take instructions and follow the directions of the woodsman and we will be freed from his fear and confusion. However, if out of false pride he rejects the instructions or even accepts them only partially, thinking himself to know better, then such a person is a fool! Therefore a devotee sees not with his eyes, but with his ears. Certainly, to see you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is very desirable, but what can we appreciate of your inconceivable pure love by our infected eyes? With our ears, though, we can hear your simple instructions as you gently encourage us along our path back to Godhead, out of this dense forest of delusion. Always your instructions ring with the urgency of not only becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious ourselves but to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others. This is Lord Caitanya's movement!

Therefore it is with great eagerness and enthusiasm that we have returned to our service, surcharged by the atmosphere of Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur, where the Lord's *līlā* is enacted, and inspired by your association and instructions.

*All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!*

Seattle devotees

## St. Louis

*Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

All glories unto you! You are the emissary of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa on this planet, and therefore we offer you our respectful obeisances again and again. You are *patita-pāvana,* the friend of the most fallen, the manifested representation of Lord Nityānanda Prabhu. As such, you are empowered to deliver unlimited fallen souls. Nityānanda Prabhu delivered Jagāi and Mādhāi, but a servant of Nityānanda Prabhu, by His Grace, can deliver thousands of Jagāi's and Mādhāi's." (*Madhya* 16.65 purport) You are delivering not thousands but millions of Jagāi's and Mādhāi's and therefore we offer you our most fallen obeisances again and again. Even after you deliver such fallen souls, you travel all over the world just to help them become enlivened in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, begging that they may get some attachment to the lotus feet of the Lord. When you are tired or not feeling well, you push on selflessly. Please help us to be of some assistance to you. Please help us to fulfill your desires. Please help us to become pure devotees. *Gurudeva kṛpā-bindu diyā, lara' ei dāse, tṛnāpekhā ati hīna:* Gurudeva, give us just one drop of mercy. Your mercy is all that we are made of.

How can we understand your mercy? Solely by your own words in your books: "Only out of His immense compassion does the Personality of Godhead reveal himself as the spiritual master...he is the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. It is worthwhile to take shelter of such a steady devotee, who is called the *āśṛaya-vigraha* or the manifestation of the form of the Lord of whom one must take shelter...the bona fide spiritual master always engages in unalloyed devotional service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead. By this test he is known to be a direct manifestation of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu. One should always think of the spiritual master in terms of his intimate relationship with Mukunda (Śrī Kṛṣṇa)...." (Cc. *Ādi.* 1.46 purport) In this way let us always try to remember you and serve without deviation.

ISKCON St. Louis

## Stockholm

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

We appeared in the darkest of ages—Kali-yuga—and were overcome by the dualities of desire and hate. Actually this material world is nothing but a part of Mahā-Viṣṇu's dream, but to us, who were born in delusion, it seemed like a hellish nightmare. The demons proclaimed this world to be the only reality and therefore there was no hope of finding a way out of this hell. Then you came, O mercy incarnation of the Lord.

*śikṣā-guruke ta 'jāni kṛṣṇera svarūpa*  
*antaryāmī, bhakta-śreṣṭha,——ei dui rūpa*

"One should know the instructing spiritual master to be the Personality of Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa manifests Himself as the Supersoul and as the greatest devotee of the Lord." (Cc. *Ādi.* 1.47) You were holding the brilliant *Bhāgavata Purāṇa* in your hand and thereby proving that in this age men shall indeed get light from this *Purāṇa.* All of a sudden we were filled with hope and joy. Now life became meaningful and we got a mission to execute. No words can describe the tremendous feeling of relief we felt when you took away our heavy burdens and when we try to thank you, you simply say that you have no mercy of your own, buy that you are simply carrying the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, just like a postman delivers letters. From the authoritative scriptures, however, we learn that spiritual life is indeed based on free will. Therefore, you must have volunteered to come to this material world to deliver all the fallen souls by distributing the message of Lord Caitanya. Doesn't this give evidence of the greatest mercy? Actually, your mercy is so great that you are giving us what we could never dream of desiring. Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as Supersoul is fulfilling all the desires of the living entities by awarding them different bodies. We have taken birth in *mleccha* families and therefore our desires were certainly very low and nasty. In spite of this, however, you have given us the highest gift, love of Kṛṣṇa. We therefore consider it impossible for us to repay our debt to you; nor can you be repayed by all the riches of the world. Therefore, we are yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we pray that we will be engaged one hundred percent in fulfilling your desires.

Now you are turning us into devotees and thus we are getting a share of Lord Kṛṣṇa opulences. Consequently we are less and less experiencing the material miseries. Therefore we must pray every day to be able to remember how we suffered in the material world, and how greatly relieved we felt when you picked us up from this ocean of birth and death, so that we can remain your menial servants and thereby perfect our lives.

*Your fallen servants in the Stockholm temple*

## Sydney

**"Oh! 'Śri Gurudeva-Śrīla Prabhupāda"**

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

You are peaceful, tolerant and forebearing.  
You are magnanimous, equipoised and always sharing.  
You are kind, honest, a real well-wisher and friend,  
pure and gentle, it was you! Kṛṣṇa did send.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

You and Lord Kṛṣṇa are united.  
Simply Kṛṣṇa words are all you have cited.  
Kṛṣṇa words and yours are one  
Kṛṣṇa has sent you to do what you have done.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

By your divine mercy from Lord Nityānanda  
this age of Kali won't drag me under.  
Everyone is suffering the material pain  
because they don't take from you the greatest gain.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Your divine and loving grace,  
mine is an extremely critical case.  
A bewildered lost and fallen soul  
rotting in a deep dark hole.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Limitless, godly empowered one,  
you don't care for what I've done.  
Yesterday is gone, tomorrow is shining,  
by your mercy I'm out of māyā's twining.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Śrī Jagad-gurudeva, Your Divine Grace,  
even though I continue in disgrace,  
you are so transcendentally kind,  
carefully giving sight to the blind.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Universal perfect master—Śrī Gurudeva  
I am destined for the grave.  
No hope other than you, Gaura Śakti.  
You are freely giving away bhakti.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

You are sane, fully controlled, mercy personified,  
descended to benedict the materially dissatisfied.  
You save the poor living entities drowning in illusion  
who are suffering death in their enjoying delusion.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Bliss, knowledge and absolute truth in your words.  
Emanating from your lips the sweetest things I have ever heard.  
Defeating all the atheists and city yogīs  
by your grace, they are revealed as bhogīs

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Bestower of real love, determined golden Lord,  
save us from Durgā's chopping sword.  
With the nectar we are always anxious for,  
you have opened wide the door.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Transcendental qualities manifest in you, magnanimous God,  
You have saved us from worshipping "god."  
Such a debt I can never repay,  
therefore I'm your servant for eternity.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Vyāsadeva you are the most royal,  
teaching all God's servants to be loyal.  
Knowledge, bliss and eternity for all,  
you have opened up the bhakti store.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Spiritual angel from Lord Keśava  
showing all who they really are.  
Killing the demoniac mentality of most,  
you, alone, are the mahā host.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Spiritual master of all universal space,  
you are perfect, expert judge of time and place.  
Those who find fault in you, don't return to tell  
of all the pain and suffering there is in hell.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Chanting the holy name, you are always found  
ecstasy in your heart rolling around,  
Cloud of mercy, in your divine word,  
auspiciousness from your lips is all that is heard.

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Leader of devotees and supreme worshipable object,  
you have inaugurated Lord Caitanya's universal sect.  
Defeating Māyāvādī sharks in the material pool  
with "It's all one—then rascal, eat stool."

O ŚRĪLA PRABHUPĀDA!

Inexhaustible, equal with the unlimited Infallible One,  
the illusory energy has stolen my tongue.  
I'm incapable of glorifying you with enough care,  
therefore please accept the following prayer:

*śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma, kevala bhakati-sadma,*  
*bando mui dāvanhāna mate*  
*yāhāra prasāde bhāi, e bhava toriyā yāi,*  
*kṛṣṇa-prāpate hoy yāhā hoite*  
*guru-mukha-padma-vākya, cittete koriyā aikya*  
*ār nā koriho mane āśā*  
*śrī-guruśaraṇe rati, ei sei uttama-gati,*  
*ye prasāde pūre sarva āśā*  
*cakhudhāna dilo yei, janme janme prabhu sei,*  
*dibya-jñān hṛde prokāśito*  
*prema-bhakti yāhā hoite, avidyā vināsa yāte,*  
*vede gāy yāhāra carito*  
*śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu, adhama janāra bandhu,*  
*lokanāth lokera jīvana*  
*hāhā prabhu koro dayā, deho more pada-chāyā,*  
*ebe yaśa ghuṣuk tribhuvana*  
*gurudeva gurudeva gurudeva*  
*gurudeva jaya jaya gurudeva*

## Tehran

*yasya prasādad bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyaprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*

This famous verse by Viśvanātha Cakravartī gives the sum and substance of Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy. Every devotee of the Lord admits his miserable position, fill of ignorance, and seeks the mercy of the Lord. In this verse Viśvanātha states that to get the mercy of the Lord, one must have the mercy of the *guru,* without whose mercy, one's spiritual life cannot be successful.

As Arjuna states in the *Bhagavad-gītā,* the minute living entity, covered over by ignorance, is bewildered about his duty. Therefore it is stated: the minute living entity, covered over by ignorance, is bewildered about his duty. Therefore it is stated:

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tahtā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

So it is essential to surrender to and get information from the *guru* regarding how to approach the supreme. The *guru* is called *śikṣa-guru* or one who instructs how to serve the divine couple—Rādhā-Govinda.

In the *Bhagavad-gītā* it states that those with resolute determination have their intelligence focused in one direction. According to Viśvanātha Cakravartī, this means that to execute the order of the spiritual master is the very life and soul of the disciple.

The disciple is no longer attached to family members, wife, children, etc., nor to money or position for his personal satisfaction, but instead uses all these for the service to his *guru.*

We devotees in Tehran pledge our lives to you Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are doing everything we can to preach your message here and distribute your books. We desire only to preach your service everywhere and we humbly beg that you keep us fixed in your service forever, even if we remain in this material world.

## Toronto

Before coming under the shelter of your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we were all gliding down to hell.

*utkrāmantaṁ sthitaṁ vāpi*  
*bhuñjānaṁ vā guṇānvitam*  
*vimūḍhā nānupaśyanti*  
*paśyanti jñāna-cakṣuṣaḥ*  
*Bhagavad-gītā 15.10*

**The foolish cannot understand how a living entity can quit his body nor can they understand what sort of body he enjoys under the spell of the modes of nature. But one whose eyes are trained in knowledge can see all this."**

*Vimūḍhāḥ* in this verse means foolish person. We were all first-class fools, engaged in sense gratification before taking shelter of your devotional service. It is stated in the *Bṛhad Aravyaka Upaniṣad:*

*yo vā etad akṣaraṁ gargy*  
*aviditvāsmāl lokāt paiti sa kṛpaṇaṇ*

**"He is a miserly man who does not solve the problems of life as a human and who thus quits this world like the cats and dogs, without understanding the science of self-realization."**

So we were paving our way to animal births in our next lives if it hadn't been for your engaging us in the loving service of the Lord. Kṛṣṇa states in the Fifteenth Chapter of the *Gītā* that for one who engages in His service, He delivers him from the ocean of birth and death. We were being trained in the Western world to be first-class sense gratifiers and now you are training us to be first-class servants of Kṛṣṇa. It is said that one cannot buy a ticket to Vṛndāvana but by serving your lotus feet we are becoming eligible in this life for going home, back to Godhead. We cannot possibly repay you for your mercy, but we can only beg you to allow us to serve you eternally. Rūpa Gosvāmī states that Lord Caitanya is the most merciful of all of Kṛṣṇa's incarnations. You are the most merciful of Lord Caitanya's representatives, for you are delivering us most fallen souls. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to your devotional service.

ISKCON New Remuṇā

## Trinidad

The shining effulgence which emanates from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and from your books is the flow of mercy which only you manifest. Some persons in this world gratify their senses unto the end of life, and others work hard to please their family, loved ones, friends, society, or humanity. But whether one engages on satisfying his own senses or whether he works to gratify others, all his material advancement must terminate with the single stroke of death. But Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is beyond the domain of death, because it is the same eternal, unending mercy which Kṛṣṇa has for the fallen soul caught within the shackles of illusion. Prabhupāda, you unlock the chains of death, and lead us to the eternal world where Kṛṣṇa reigns supreme.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are qualified to deliver all living entities. There is no amount of gold worth even one moment's association with you.

We can never know you. We have been in darkness so long we are almost blind. And you are dazzling like the sun. You bring life to us.

Tarakśya dāsa adhikārī and all the assembled devotees

## Vancouver

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda*

In the age of Kali, men become like dogs, fighting and snarling over the bones of material sense objects. Although human life is meant for elevation of the self away from selfishness and greed, towards greed's opposite, pure live, in this age even the process of elevation—religion—becomes an acceleration for the degradation of human consciousness. As a result, human life has become a hopeless, confused nightmare, as humans frantically try to put out the fire of anxiety by dousing the flames with the gasoline of sense gratification. Escalating this desperate condition are the fools posing as educators, politicians and philosophers, who lead the path on the road to hell, waving the flags of atheism, impersonalism, and voidism as their standards. This is actually the condition of the world today. The glitter of the steel and glass skyscrapers blinds the world to the misery of those living within. All discrimination, truth, and reality are rapidly disappearing from the world, as the leaders extort the people to forget God, like a myth, and worship the television and rocketship as ultimate reality.

In the *Bhagavad-gītā* Kṛṣṇa promises that he will intervene when all truth and hope are in danger of being overwhelmed by illusion and despair. Kṛṣṇa's promise to protect His devotees has been proven again and again in pastimes described in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.* This promise is now being fulfilled by Kṛṣṇa, and the personification of this promise is Śrīla Prabhupāda, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami. Śrīla Prabhupāda is obviously the direct representative of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Lord Caitanya was so kind that he killed the demons by flooding the world in the ocean of nectar and drowning them in *kṛṣṇa-prema.* Lord Caitanya made the process of religion all-attractive, even to the materialists whose only thoughts are sense gratification. Lord Caitanya distributed His mercy so profusely that even tigers and deer would dance together in ecstasy. Śrīla Prabhupāda is also flooding the whole world with this mercy so that dogs and cats like ourselves are now dancing together, swooning in ecstasy and forgetting our differences. You are uniting us in the purpose of satisfying Kṛṣṇa and in this way removing all chaos from our lives.

In this age, when faith in God has declined to practically nil, Śrīla Prabhupāda is singlehandedly pushing back this tide of atheism. Śrīla Prabhupāda is fighting this battle of atheism on all fronts with logic, philosophy, anecdotes and stories that surpass any of the feeble arguments of the atheists. His kindness, magnanimity, warmth and affection can conquer even the most dubious heart. His scholarship, perseverance and brilliance can conquer even the most dubious heart. His scholarship, perseverance and brilliance are beating back the tides of impersonalism and voidism. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are just dimly able to perceive your greatness, but even that small understanding is filling us with the greatest awe and respect, and we are simply praying that we can, by Kṛṣṇa's grace, properly appreciate your most elevated position and become bona fide disciples. A bona fide disciple is one who is always obeying his every instruction, and who is fully accepting his spiritual master's desire as his life and soul. Our only desire is that Kṛṣṇa will give us the intelligence to become your bona fide disciples so that we can somehow please you, as this is the only desire we now have. We at Vancouver temple are looking forward to another year to dedicate ourselves in your service and hope to expand the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in a way that will please Your Divine Grace.

ISKCON Vancouver

## Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandira

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate-bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śunyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Every morning we are rising very early to take bath, apply *tilaka* to the body and attend *maṅgala-ārati.* And we sing:

*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-*  
*trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam*

The material world is compared to a revolving wheel of birth and death, *saṁsāra,* and we fallen souls of this world of birth and death are stated to be seated on a machine made of material energy, *yantrārūḍhāni māyayā* (Bg. 18.61), traveling from one body to another, one species to another, enjoying or suffering according to our *karma:*

*dehino 'smin yathā dehe*  
*kaumāraṁ yauvanaṁ jarā*  
*tathā denāntara-prāptir*  
*dhīras tatra na muhyati*

**"As the embodied soul continually passes, in this body, from boyhood to youth to old age, the soul similarly passes into another body at death. The self-realized soul is not bewildered by such a change." (Bg. 2.13)**

The word *dehinaḥ* refers to those who are embodied. These bodies are destined to suffer disease, old age and death, and then after death we must again me stuffed in another womb to be bitten by tiny worms, living in our own stool and urine, and doubled over for want of more space. In these ways we are suffering miserably. Therefore the material world is likened to a blazing forest fire of birth and death, *saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka.*

But in Śrī Vṛndāvana Dhāma, the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa displayed His wonderful pastimes with the cowherd men and women, full of eternity, knowledge and unlimited bliss, just to attract the conditioned souls captivated by the illusory enjoyment of the material world. And from that transcendental land of Vṛndāvana one of the Lord's most confidential servitor has come directly empowered by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, because without the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu no one can spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. He began to preach *sanātana-dharma,* the eternal occupation of the soul, loving service to Kṛṣṇa, the all-attractive Supreme Personality of Godhead. Now we can see the practical result. From all over the world—America, Canada, Mexico, Brazil and the rest of South America, London, Paris, Iran, Spain, India, Africa, Australia, Japan and China—people are surrendering their lives and becoming devotees of Kṛṣṇa. What other person has started with forty rupees and in ten years established an international society for God consciousness with centers in every major city in the world?

Now by the grace of that confidential servitor we have been given the opportunity to visit and live in the wonderful land of Śrī Goloka-Vṛndāvana, where the very dust is worshipped by great sages, due to its having been touched by the beautiful lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. And this pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa has also established a beautiful temple of Kṛṣṇa and His brother Balarāma on this land, in the very place where Their lotus feet pressed into the ground as They played with Their cows and cowherd boyfriends in the forest of Raman Reti.

That person is none other than Your Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇu-Pāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācāra Aṣṭottara Śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, founder-*ācārya* of the great International Society for Krishna Consciousness. By your grace one can cross over the miseries of the material world and obtain the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. Therefore, we at the Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandira, Raman Reti, Vṛndāvana, India, humbly offer our respectful obeisances to you, falling on the ground at your lotus feet. The lotus feet of our spiritual master are the only way that we can attain pure devotional service, You are the ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor and the lord and master of the devotees. Give us the shade of your lotus feet. Your fame is spread all over the three worlds.

*Praying this meets you in good health,*
